```json
{
  "text": "## Reviewer 2 — Round 1 审稿报告\n\n**论文**: \"Depth Profiling: Classifying Recursive Generation Depth in Base LLM Text Chains\"\n**目标 venue**: EMNLP 2026 (ARR Long Paper)\n**审查侧重**: 新颖性、Related Work 定位、Framing 一致性、写作质量、格式合规\n\n---\n\n## 总体评分\n\n**Overall Assessment**: 3.0 (Findings)\n**Confidence**: 4/5 (Quite sure)\n**一句话总结**: 实验方法严谨、负面结果诚实报告，但新颖性受并行工作和 base-model-only 限制削弱，Introduction 的动机叙事与实际发现之间存在显著张力。\n\n---\n\n## 官方维度打分（ACL ARR Review Form）\n\n| 维度 | 分数 | 理由摘要 |\n|------|------|----------|\n| **Soundness** | 3.5/5 | 统计方法严谨（bootstrap CI、TOST、多种 effect-size 度量），但 DDR partition 的边界案例（tail decay 4/8 通过、entropy min 需要 domain-informed 干预）和 framing 不一致削弱了整体 soundness |\n| **Excitement** | 2.5/5 | 任务定义新颖但增量有限（binary → 3-class）；三篇独立工作收敛于 ~3 classes 削弱了本文独特性；base-model-only scope 和负面 downstream 结果限制了实际影响力 |\n| **Reproducibility** | 4/5 | 详细的超参数、软件版本、GPU 配置（62 GPU-hours, 4×RTX 5090）、承诺发布代码和数据 |\n| **Confidence** | 4/5 | 熟悉 AI 文本检测、分布漂移和统计方法学领域 |\n\n---\n\n## Must-Fix 问题（影响接收决定）\n\n### N1 [critical] Framing 矛盾：Introduction 承诺 vs. 实际发现\n\n- **具体位置**: Introduction §1 (line 5) vs. Discussion §5 (line 16) vs. Appendix M (downstream)\n- **问题**: Introduction 声称 \"downstream implications for model collapse theory and data provenance\"（§1, line 5），但论文实际发现与此不符：\n  - Discussion §5 承认 depth profiling 是 \"diagnostic rather than prescriptive role\"（line 16）\n  - 下游实验（Appendix M, Table 14）显示 depth-aware curation 与 random subsampling 统计等价（TOST p = 1.1×10⁻⁴），深度信息不改善训练质量\n  - Discussion §5 line 7 明确说 DDR 是 \"parameter-dependent phenomenon, not an intrinsic property of recursive generation\"\n  - Instruction-tuned 模型（实际部署主流）信号严重退化（OLMo LOGO F1: 0.763 → 0.338, Appendix K Table 11）\n- **为什么影响接收**: Introduction 的叙事建立在 \"data provenance\" 和 \"model collapse theory\" 的实用动机上，但论文最终证明这些应用方向不成立。这导致论文的故事线在 Introduction 和 Discussion 之间断裂。审稿人会质问：如果 depth 信息既不能改善数据筛选，又不适用于 instruction-tuned 模型，这项工作的实际价值在哪里？\n- **建议修改方向**: 重写 Introduction 的动机部分，将论文定位为 \"理解递归生成的分布结构\" 的科学发现，而非面向实际应用的工具。将 data provenance/model collapse 从核心动机降级为远期联系。承认 base-model-only 是当前方法的根本限制而非可延伸的初步结果。\n\n### N2 [critical] 新颖性受并行工作和增量性质削弱\n\n- **具体位置**: Introduction §1 (Contribution list), Related Work §2.2 (lines 27-31), Discussion §5 (全文)\n- **问题**:\n  1. **三篇独立收敛**: Broken Telephone (ACL 2025)、Markovian Chains (arXiv 2026) 与本文均收敛于 ~3 个可区分的深度类别。Related Work §2.2 line 31 自己承认 \"all three works independently converge on approximately three distinguishable classes\"。虽然本文的 DDR 分解（what converges vs what stays stable）是独特角度，但三篇并行工作的存在极大削弱了 \"generation depth classification\" 作为新任务的新颖性声明。\n  2. **DDR 是描述性框架而非科学发现**: 论文自己说 \"DDR is an empirical descriptive framework, not a claimed universal law\"（Discussion §5 line 6）。在仅有 7 个预选特征上的 tri-partition 属于分析工具而非发现。\n  3. **数据驱动验证不完全支持 3/1/3**: k-means 最优结构是 k=2（silhouette=0.632），k=3 的 silhouette 降至 0.499 且 entropy min 聚错类（Appendix S Table 16）。Holdout split 产出 3/2/2 而非 3/1/3（Appendix S）。需要 domain-informed binary-task d 才能恢复 3/1/3，这削弱了 \"数据驱动\" 的声称。\n  4. **Binary → 3-class 增量有限**: 从 binary 检测扩展到 3-class ordinal classification 是自然演进，Beyond Binary (Cheng et al., 2025) 已提出 4-role 分类（虽然轴不同），SemEval 2024 Task 8 包含多源归因。\n- **为什么影响接收**: EMNLP 2026 标准下，empirical contribution 需要足够的新颖性和影响力。当三篇并行工作得出相似结论时，本文的独特贡献收窄至 DDR 分解本身。而 DDR 是 post-hoc descriptive framework（作者自认），在 7 个特征上的分区不具有广泛影响力。\n- **建议修改方向**: (a) 更诚实地降低新颖性声称：不要说 \"No existing method addresses this task\"（Introduction line 9），而应说 \"No existing method provides a feature-level decomposition...\"（将 novelty 锚定在 DDR 分解上而非任务本身）。(b) 在 Related Work 中更深入对比三篇工作的异同，说明本文的增量贡献具体在哪里。(c) 考虑在 Discussion 中讨论三篇工作收敛于 ~3 classes 的 meta-level 意义，这本身可能是一个有趣的发现。\n\n### N3 [critical] Base-model-only scope 严重限制论文影响力\n\n- **具体位置**: Title, Abstract (line 7), Introduction (line 14), Appendix K (Table 11), Limitations §7\n- **问题**: 论文标题 \"Depth Profiling: Classifying Recursive Generation Depth in Base LLM Text Chains\" 包含了 \"Base\" 限定，Abstract line 7 也声明了限制。但这个限制不仅是实验范围的约束，而是方法论的根本局限：\n  - Instruction-tuned 模型（OLMo-Instruct LOGO F1=0.338）的信号退化不是渐进的而是灾难性的\n  - Entropy-mean ρ 符号反转（base: -0.60 → instruct: +0.48）表明 RLHF 根本改变了 DDR 的底层机制\n  - 实际 web 文本几乎全部由 instruction-tuned/RLHF 模型生成\n  - 在 Limitations §7 中 Qwen 模型 length-matched 后 binary F1≈0.5（接近随机）\n- **为什么影响接收**: 一个仅适用于 base 模型的 depth profiling 方法在 2026 年的实际意义极为有限。论文的 data provenance 动机隐含假设方法可推广至实际场景，但 instruct 实验证明这不可行。这将论文的贡献限定为一个受控实验室环境中的科学观察，而非可部署的工具——但论文的叙事未完全反映这一定位。\n- **建议修改方向**: 明确将论文定位为 \"understanding base-model recursive dynamics\" 的科学贡献，在 Introduction 和 Contribution 列表中降低实用性声称。或者，显著扩展 instruct 实验：如果能找到部分 instruct 设置下信号可恢复的条件（如特定 prompt 类型或 adaptation 方法），将大幅增加论文价值。\n\n---\n\n## Should-Fix 问题（影响评分但不一票否决）\n\n### W1 [warning] DDR partition 边界案例处理不够 robust\n\n- **具体位置**: Results §4.2 (lines 64-73), Appendix S (Tables 16-17), Appendix O (Table 15)\n- **问题**: DDR tri-partition 的核心声称是 3/1/3 分区，但多个验证分析不完全支持：\n  - Tail decay 在 TOST 中仅 4/8 generators 通过 ε=0.20（Table 15），四个未通过的 generators 显示 d=0.15-0.22\n  - Entropy min 的 pooled |ρ|=0.40 远高于其他 near-stationary 特征（<0.10），需要 binary-task d 才能归入 near-stationary\n  - Holdout split 产出 3/2/2（两个 intermediate），不是宣称的 3/1/3（Table 17, Appendix S line 90）\n  - k-means clustering 最优 k=2 而非 k=3，3/1/3 需要 domain-informed refinement（Appendix S line 23）\n- **建议**: 考虑将分区描述为 \"primarily a 2-group structure (drifting vs non-drifting) with nuanced boundary features\" 而非坚持 3/1/3 为 DDR 的核心发现。或者更明确地说明 3/1/3 是一个 domain-informed refinement（而非 data-driven partition）。\n\n### W2 [warning] 3-class F1 在 Table 1 中的呈现可能误导\n\n- **具体位置**: Results §4.1 (Table 1), Abstract (line 6)\n- **问题**: Table 1 同时显示 3-class F1 (0.762) 和 binary F1 (0.694)。虽然 caption 注明了 gen-0 sharing inflation，但 3-class F1 作为第一个数值列仍可能误导快速浏览的读者。Abstract line 4 中 \"three classes\" 的强调也可能让读者期待 0.762 是主要结果。\n- **建议**: 在 Table 1 中将 binary F1 放在 3-class F1 之前，或将 3-class 列标题改为 \"3-cl F1†\" 带上更醒目的 footnote。Abstract 应报告 binary F1 而非仅提及三类分类。（注：Abstract 已报告 binary F1=0.694，这一点做得好。）\n\n### W3 [warning] 多个 generator subsets 使结果难以交叉对比\n\n- **具体位置**: Method §3.4 (line 69), Appendix 各处\n- **问题**: 论文在不同实验中使用 5-gen、6-gen、8-gen subsets（Method §3.4 line 69 明确说明了三个 subsets）。这导致：\n  - QLoRA 实验使用 6 generators（Appendix L），主实验用 8 generators\n  - 5-class LOGO 使用 6 generators（Appendix D Table 5）\n  - Cross-model chains 使用 5 generators（Results §4.1 footnote 1）\n  - Instruct 对比使用 \"earlier LOGO subsets (5-6 generators)\"（Appendix K line 4）\n  - 读者难以在不同实验间进行横向比较\n- **建议**: 在可能的范围内统一到 8-generator 评估。如果部分实验因历史原因无法更新，至少在 Appendix U (Generator Subset Summary) 中提供一个清晰的对照表标明每个实验使用哪些 generators。（注：Appendix U 已存在但信息密度可更高。）\n\n### W4 [warning] Classification performance 上限偏低\n\n- **具体位置**: Table 1, Table 2, Results §4\n- **问题**: 主要指标 binary F1=0.694（LOGO），per-generator 范围 0.633-0.724。这意味着约 30% 的 gen-1/gen-2+ 区分错误。虽然论文定位为 \"empirical contribution\" 而非方法，但 0.694 的性能难以支撑 corpus-level depth auditing 的实用场景（Discussion §5 line 16）。Detection baseline 的 4-detector+surface ensemble (0.708) 甚至略优于 7-feature set (0.705)（Table 2），表明现有方法已隐式编码了 depth 信息。\n- **建议**: 在 Discussion 中更坦率地讨论 0.694 F1 对实际 corpus-level auditing 的含义。LOGO 是强评估协议，但 0.694 在实际部署中的信噪比可能不足。考虑在 Limitations 中增加对 performance ceiling 的讨论。\n\n### W5 [warning] Contribution 3 叙事过于笼统\n\n- **具体位置**: Introduction §1 (Contribution 3, line 22)\n- **问题**: Contribution 3 声称 \"comprehensive evaluation spanning temperature sensitivity, prompt-type dependence, instruction-tuning degradation, cross-domain transfer, and downstream training utility, including negative results\"。这是一个 catch-all，涵盖了约 15 个 appendix sections。对于 empirical paper 来说，这个 contribution 过于模糊——具体的发现是什么？\n- **建议**: 将 Contribution 3 分解为 2-3 个具体发现（如 \"DDR degrades under instruction tuning with consistent sign reversal of entropy-mean ρ\" 和 \"depth-aware curation provides no advantage over random subsampling\"），每个绑定到具体 section。\n\n---\n\n## 小问题（不影响接收）\n\n1. **Abstract 中 \"LLM\" 未定义**: line 3 \"base (pretrained) LLMs\" 首次出现但未展开 Large Language Model。ARR 模板要求 Abstract 内定义所有缩写。\n2. **Table 2 的 ms/text 列**: Avg-LogProb、FastDetectGPT、LD-Score、Binoculars 均标 \"N/A\"——为什么？这些都是可计时的方法。如果是因为不使用相同 pipeline，应注明原因。\n3. **Figure 1 字体大小**: 视觉审查显示 axis labels 和 legend 约 6-7pt，接近 ACL 可读性下限。\n4. **Orphan bib entry**: `self_refine` (Madaan et al., 2023) 在 references.bib 中但未被引用。应删除或引用。\n5. **Em-dash 检查**: 仅出现在 LaTeX 注释中（% 行），正文清洁。✓\n6. **En-dash 检查**: 无解释性 en-dash。✓\n7. **AI 写作模式**: 未发现明显的 -ing 结尾短语堆积、过度分段、\"Furthermore/Moreover\" 连续使用。写作质量总体良好。\n8. [Round 1 审稿请求]:
```
## Step 2 交叉分享 — 请逐条回应 Reviewer 1 的审稿意见

以下是 Reviewer 1 的完整审稿意见。请逐条回应每个 Must-Fix 和 Should-Fix 问题：
- **[AGREE]** 同意并说明理由
- **[REBUT]** 反驳并提供证据（cite 具体 Section/Table/Figure）

---

### Reviewer 1 的 Must-Fix 问题

**N1** [critical] DDR 分区阈值的统计基础不可靠：异方差下使用 Cohen's d
- §4.2 报告 Levene tests reject equal-variance (p < 10^{-10})，但 DDR 三分结构的核心定义依赖 Cohen's d 阈值（drifting: d > 0.75; near-stationary: d < 0.10）。在方差显著不等的条件下，Cohen's d 是有偏估计量。建议使用 Glass's Δ 或 Cliff's delta。

**N2** [critical] Cohen's d 阈值存在 post-hoc 选择嫌疑
- K-means 验证显示 2-cluster silhouette=0.632 但 3-cluster silhouette=0.499。3-cluster 结构弱于 2-cluster。Holdout 4/4 split 产出 3/2/2 而非 3/1/3。论文用 "domain-informed refinement" 事后调整。

**N3** [critical] Gen-0 seed sharing 导致 3-class headline 指标潜在膨胀
- Gen-0 per-class F1=0.94 接近 trivial。独立 seed 实验仅覆盖 2/8 generators。Binary F1=0.694 才是 leak-free 数字。建议将 binary F1 作为 abstract 和 §4.1 的 primary metric。

**N4** [critical] 下游 TOST 等价性检验统计效力严重不足（n=3）
- Cross-seed std < 0.001，Cohen's d=86.0/119.7 明显虚高。n=3 的自由度仅为 4，检验功效不足。Margin sensitivity analysis 显示 ±1% margin 全部 fail。

**N5** [critical] QLoRA 大幅超越手工特征，削弱 DDR 框架贡献
- QLoRA 3-class F1=0.905 vs XGBoost 0.762（+14.3pp）。手工特征是 "strong but lossy compression"。DDR 三分法可能不反映深度信号的真实结构。

### Reviewer 1 的 Should-Fix 问题

**W1** 温度敏感性严重限制实用性（t=0.9 下降 9.3-26.0pp，可靠范围仅 t∈[0.5,0.7]）
**W2** Ordinal classification framing 与 nominal XGBoost classifier 不一致
**W3** TTR-hapax 高相关（r=0.97）与"有效维度~5"论述不充分
**W4** Generator 规模局限（≤8B），结果显示规模效应（Mistral-7B binary F1=0.633 最低）
**W5** Cross-domain 实验存在温度混淆（Academic domain t=0.7 binary F1 仅 0.628）
**W6** 特征选择缺乏系统性（未考虑句法复杂度、语义连贯性等替代特征）

请对以上每条给出 [AGREE] 或 [REBUT]，并说明理由。

52	Features exhibit \emph{differential depth response} (DDR) with a three-way partition: \emph{drifting} features (entropy mean, TTR, hapax ratio) shift monotonically with depth; entropy std occupies a sole \emph{intermediate} position (large level shift, low monotonicity); and \emph{near-stationary} features (entropy max, tail decay, entropy min) show negligible depth effects (Cohen's $d < 0.10$ vs.\ $d > 0.75$ for drifting features; CV shift $<$3\% vs.\ $>$15\%).

1	\section{Per-Generator Equivalence Testing}
2	\label{app:tost}
3	
4	To confirm that the near-stationary classification is not a pooling artifact, we apply two one-sided $t$-tests (TOST) \cite{lakens_equivalence} for each feature within each generator separately (gen-1 vs.\ gen-2+, equivalence bound $|d| < 0.20$).
5	We adopt Cohen's conventional small effect size ($d{=}0.20$) \cite{cohen1988statistical} as the smallest effect of practical significance (SESOI): effects below this threshold are unlikely to be practically meaningful for depth discrimination.
6	LOFO analysis independently supports this choice: removing any near-stationary feature changes classification F1 by $\leq$1.5\,pp (Appendix~\ref{app:lofo}), linking the statistical threshold to classification irrelevance.
7	Sensitivity analysis at $\varepsilon \in \{0.15, 0.20, 0.25\}$ confirms the partition is robust to the bound choice (see DDR Validation appendix).
8	Table~\ref{tab:tost-per-gen} reports observed $|d|$; \checkmark{} marks features for which the TOST null hypothesis (non-equivalence) is rejected at $\alpha = 0.05$.
9	
10	Entropy min passes equivalence for all eight generators; entropy max passes for six of eight (exceeding $\varepsilon$ only for Pythia-1B and Pythia-2.8B, where $|d| \approx 0.21$--$0.25$); tail decay passes for four of eight.
11	All drifting and intermediate features fail equivalence uniformly, with $|d|$ ranging from 0.23 (entropy std, Mistral) to 1.40 (entropy mean, Pythia-1B).
12	The partition is robust at the individual-generator level, with entropy min as the most consistently stationary feature.
13	Figure~\ref{fig:ddr-scatter} visualizes the DDR partition in ($|\rho|$, $d$) space, showing a clear gap between the three groups.

9	\midrule
10	entropy\_max    & 0.774 & $-$1.50\,pp \\
11	entropy\_mean   & 0.783 & $-$0.63\,pp \\
12	ttr             & 0.784 & $-$0.55\,pp \\
13	hapax\_ratio    & 0.785 & $-$0.42\,pp \\
14	tail\_decay     & 0.786 & $-$0.33\,pp \\
15	entropy\_std    & 0.786 & $-$0.29\,pp \\
16	entropy\_min    & 0.789 & $+$0.01\,pp \\
17	\midrule
18	Full (7 features) & 0.789 & --- \\
19	\bottomrule
20	\end{tabular}
21	\caption{Leave-one-feature-out ablation (6-gen LOGO). Each row drops one feature and re-evaluates with the LOGO protocol. Sorted by impact magnitude.}
22	\label{tab:lofo}
23	\end{table}
24	
25	\paragraph{Analysis.}
26	Entropy\_max causes the largest degradation when removed ($-$1.5\,pp), despite ranking only fourth in permutation importance (split frequency 0.063). This indicates that entropy\_max provides unique signal (capturing the most surprising token under the reference model) that is not redundant with other features.
27	Conversely, entropy\_min is fully redundant ($\Delta$\,=\,+0.01\,pp), consistent with its near-zero permutation importance (0.015).
28	Six of seven features contribute positively to the ensemble, which confirms that the 7-feature design is not over-specified.
29

1	\section{Independent-Seed Control}
2	\label{app:seed-control}
3	
4	To rule out topic-level leakage from shared seeds across generators, we construct an independent-seed evaluation where Pythia-2.8B and OLMo-2-1B use completely disjoint Wikipedia seed pools (500 seeds each, zero overlap).
5	Each generator produces depth-0 through depth-4 chains on its own seed pool; features are extracted with the same Pythia-410M reference model.
6	We then train a 7-feature XGBoost classifier on one generator's data and test on the other, in both directions.
7	
8	\begin{table}[h]
9	\centering
10	\small
11	\begin{tabular}{lcc}
12	\toprule
13	\textbf{Setting} & \textbf{Binary F1} & \textbf{95\% CI} \\
14	\midrule
15	Pythia-2.8B $\rightarrow$ OLMo-2-1B & 0.695 & [0.673, 0.717] \\
16	OLMo-2-1B $\rightarrow$ Pythia-2.8B & 0.666 & [0.643, 0.689] \\
17	\midrule
18	Independent-seed average & 0.680 & --- \\
19	Shared-seed LOGO (reference) & 0.694 & [0.688, 0.700] \\
20	\bottomrule
21	\end{tabular}
22	\caption{Independent-seed cross-generator evaluation (binary: gen-1 vs.\ gen-2+). Disjoint seed pools eliminate any possibility of topic-based leakage. The average independent-seed F1 (0.680) is within 1.4\,pp of the shared-seed LOGO baseline (0.694), confirming that the classifier captures generative degradation patterns rather than seed-specific topic content.}
23	\label{tab:seed-control}
24	\end{table}
25	
26	The small gap ($-$1.4\,pp) between independent-seed and shared-seed evaluation falls within bootstrap confidence intervals, and both directions individually exceed 0.66.
27	Combined with the seed-level demeaning control (\S\ref{sec:results-classification}), these results provide converging evidence that depth classification is driven by distributional properties of recursive generation, not topic identity.
28

Abstract (line 6) 已经报告 binary F1=0.694 作为主要指标\n2. §4.1 line 8-9 明确声明 \"we therefore adopt leak-free binary F1 (gen-1 vs gen-2+) = 0.694 as the primary metric throughout\"\n3. 除 independent-seed 外，还有 seed-level demeaning (F1=0.701, §4.1 line 23) 提供额外证据\n\n因此 R1 的建议（将 binary F1 作为 Abstract 和 §4.1 的 primary metric）**论文已经基本做到了**。但我同意 Table 1 中 3-class F1 作为第一个数值列仍可能误导读者（这也是我的 W2 观察）。Independent-seed 仅覆盖 2/8 generators 确实是一个局限——但 2 generators 跨越不同 families (Pythia vs OLMo) 且结果一致 (F1=0.666-0.695)，这提供了合理的佐证。\n\n**我的判断**: 这是 Should-Fix 而非 Must-Fix。论文已有充分的 leak-free 评估框架，Table 1 的列顺序调整即可解决剩余问题。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R1-N4: 下游 TOST 统计效力不足 (n=3)**\n\n这是一个有力的统计批评。n=3 seeds 意味着 df=4，TOST 的统计功效极低。Cohen's d = 86.0/119.7 显然是由 cross-seed std < 0.001 造成的 artifact（论文自己在 Appendix M line 70 也承认 \"unreliable\"）。\n\n**关键证据**: Appendix M line 73 论文自己说 \"with only n=3 seeds per condition, the TOST results have limited generalizability: the test is valid given observed variance but the conclusion generalizes over only three random seeds. We characterize this as a preliminary observation rather than a definitive null result.\"\n\nR1 的批评方向正确。但论文的自我限定（\"preliminary observation\"）部分缓解了过度声称的风险。**核心问题是 n=3 是否足以支撑 \"depth-aware curation 无用\" 这一被推广到 Discussion §5 line 16 的结论**。Discussion 将此结果用于支持 \"diagnostic rather than prescriptive role\" 的定位——这个推广超出了 n=3 实验的支撑范围。\n\n**我的建议**: 与 R1 一致，建议增加 seeds 或在 Discussion 中限定此结论的初步性质。\n\n---\n\n**[REBUT] R1-N5: QLoRA 削弱 DDR 框架贡献**\n\n这是我与 R1 **分歧最大**的一条。R1 认为 QLoRA 3-class F1=0.905 大幅超越 XGBoost 0.762（实际是 0.789，6-gen baseline；8-gen 为 0.762），因此 DDR 框架的贡献被削弱。\n\n**我的反驳理由**：\n\n1. **DDR 和 QLoRA 回答不同问题**。DDR 回答 \"递归生成中什么在变化、什么保持稳定\"，QLoRA 回答 \"能否最大化分类精度\"。二者不是竞争关系。论文 Discussion §5 line 18 明确说：\"the handcrafted ensemble trades classification ceiling for interpretability, enabling the DDR decomposition that end-to-end classifiers cannot provide.\" 这个定位是合理的。\n\n2. **QLoRA 的存在实际上支持了论文的核心声称**。如果 QLoRA 也表现差，反而会质疑 depth signal 是否真实存在。QLoRA F1=0.905 证实了 depth signal 是 real and exploitable（Appendix L line 33），而 7 个手工特征是 lossy compression——这恰恰使 DDR 的 \"哪些维度在漂移\" 这个问题更有价值。\n\n3. **实用性论点**。DDR 不需要 GPU inference，仅需一个 410M reference model (905 MB)，而 QLoRA 需要 fine-tune 一个 LLM。在 corpus-level audit 场景中，interpretable lightweight features 有独立价值。\n\n4. **R1 的隐含假设值得质疑**: \"DDR 三分法可能不反映深度信号的真实结构\" 这个论点意味着只有 end-to-end 学到的表示才能反映\"真实结构\"。但 DDR 的价值恰在于可解释性——它告诉我们 *为什么* 递归生成导致分布变化（location shift 而非 shape change），而 QLoRA 是 black box。\n\n**我的判断**: 这不应是 Must-Fix。QLoRA 与 DDR 是互补的而非竞争的。论文应在 Discussion 中更清楚地阐述这一点（当前已有但可以加强），但 QLoRA 的存在不削弱 DDR 的解释性贡献。将此降为 Should-Fix 级别。\n\n---\n\n### Should-Fix 逐条回应\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W1: 温度敏感性限制实用性**\n\n完全同意。可靠范围 t∈[0.5, 0.7] 对实用性是严重限制。Table 13（Appendix H）显示 t=0.9 时所有 4 个 generators 退化 9.3-26.0pp，且 Pythia-2.8B 降至 0.480（接近随机）。这意味着 DDR 假设低-中温度的 \"standard decoding regime\"，而实际部署中温度选择多样。\n\n---\n\n**[REBUT] R1-W2: Ordinal framing 与 nominal XGBoost 不一致**\n\n论文在 Appendix H (appendix_classifier.tex line 31) 已直接回应了这个问题：\"Ordinal-specific methods (CORAL, Frank-Hall) achieve comparable F1 with overlapping CIs, which confirms that 3-class granularity provides insufficient resolution for ranking-aware losses to improve over standard multiclass classification.\"\n\n5-class LOGO 也同样验证（Appendix D line 50）: \"Frank-Hall ordinal reduction (0.451)\" vs XGBoost (0.501)，ordinal methods 不优于 nominal。Ordinal framing 是对 *任务* 的概念化（depth 有自然序），不要求使用 ordinal-specific classifier——尤其当后者不带来性能提升时。\n\n这不应是 Should-Fix。论文已有充分的实验证据证明 ordinal classifier 无优势，task framing 和 classifier choice 是独立决策。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE with nuance] R1-W3: TTR-hapax r=0.97 与有效维度讨论**\n\nR1 正确指出 effective dimensionality ≈ 5 的讨论不够充分。但论文在 §4.2 line 78-79 已有声明：\"TTR and hapax ratio are strongly correlated (r = 0.97), reducing effective dimensionality to ≈5; both are retained for marginal LOFO gains.\"\n\nLOFO 数据（Appendix I Table 10）显示 dropping hapax only costs 0.42pp，dropping TTR costs 0.55pp——marginal gains。论文的选择（保留二者）可以理解为 \"宁多不少\"，但确实应讨论是否 5-feature subset 是更 parsimonious 的表述。\n\n**我的建议**: 在 Method 或 Discussion 中加一句说明 \"a 5-feature subset excluding one of TTR/hapax would be equally valid for the DDR partition\" 即可。这不是 structural 问题。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W4: Generator 规模局限 ≤8B**\n\n同意，这是重要限制。Table 1 显示明确的 scale effect：Mistral-7B (0.633) 和 Qwen3-8B (0.692) 显著低于 ≤2.8B generators (0.695-0.724)。论文在 Limitations §7 line 12 承认 \"Frontier models (70B+) and closed-source models remain untested; their higher fluency may further compress feature margins.\" 这是 honest acknowledgement 但不够——scale degradation 的趋势暗示 DDR 在更大模型上可能完全消失，这对论文的科学价值有直接影响。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W5: Cross-domain 温度混淆**\n\n完全同意。论文自己在 Appendix J line 5 标注了 \"Important caveat: Cross-domain generation used t=1.0, top-p=1.0, which falls outside the method's reliable operating range.\" 这是一个设计缺陷（虽然论文补充了 t=0.7 数据），核心问题是 domain effect 和 temperature effect 被混淆了。论文诚实标注了这一点，但这确实削弱了 cross-domain generalization 的 claim 强度。\n\n---\n\n**[REBUT with partial agree] R1-W6: 特征选择缺乏系统性**\n\nR1 建议考虑句法复杂度、语义连贯性等替代特征。论文 Method §3.3 line 61 坦诚说 \"The seven features were selected a priori from two complementary perspectives; no larger candidate set was explored.\"\n\n**反驳部分**: 论文的核心贡献是 DDR 分解，不是特征工程。7 个特征来自两个互补视角（reference-model NLL + surface lexical diversity），这是 principled selection 而非 arbitrary。Method §3.3 line 35 解释：\"We use interpretable distributional statistics rather than learned representations so that the DDR tri-partition remains transparent.\" 如果加入更多特征（如句法树深度、语义连贯性），DDR 分解的 dimensionality 和 interpretability 会变得更复杂，不一定更好。\n\n**同意部分**: 从 robustness 角度说，如果论文声称 DDR 是递归生成的 *general* property，那么应在更丰富的特征空间中验证。当前 7 个特征可能遗漏了不同行为模式的维度。论文应至少讨论为什么未探索其他特征类型。\n\n---\n\n### 总结\n\n| R1 编号 | 我的判断 | 理由概要 |\n|---------|---------|---------|\n| N1 | AGREE (降级 Should-Fix) | Cohen's d 偏差合理但 multi-metric convergence 缓解；补充 robust effect size 即可 |\n| N2 | AGREE (Critical) | 与我的 W1 一致；3/1/3 是 domain-informed refinement 不是 data-driven discovery |\n| N3 | AGREE (降级 Should-Fix) | 论文已采用 binary F1 为主；Table 列顺序调整即可 |\n| N4 | AGREE (Critical) | n=3 不足以支撑推广到 Discussion 的结论 |\n| N5 | REBUT | DDR 和 QLoRA 回答不同问题；QLoRA 支持而非削弱 DDR |\n| W1 | AGREE | 温度限制是真实问题 |\n| W2 | REBUT | Appendix H 已证明 ordinal classifier 无优势 |\n| W3 | AGREE (minor) | 可加一句说明 5-feature subset equally valid |\n| W4 | AGREE | Scale degradation 趋势需更多讨论 |\n| W5 | AGREE | 温度与 domain 混淆，论文已标注但仍是缺陷 |\n| W6 | REBUT (partial) | DDR 的价值在于 interpretable features，但应讨论为何未探索其他类型 |",
```json
  "reviewer_id": "2",
  "project": "gen-depth-fingerprint"
}
```

_source line 2050 · 2026-05-19T02:54:31.187265+00:00_

交叉回应已提交。

**与 R1 的主要共识**：N2（3/1/3 partition post-hoc 选择）和 N4（TOST n=3 统计效力不足）——两位 reviewer 独立得出相同结论，增强可信度。

**与 R1 的主要分歧**：
- **N5 (QLoRA 削弱 DDR)** — 我认为 DDR 和 QLoRA 回答不同问题（理解 vs. 性能），QLoRA 的优越性反而验证了 depth signal 的真实性，不削弱 DDR 的解释性价值。
- **N1 和 N3** — R1 定为 Critical，我建议降级为 Should-Fix：N1 因为 multi-metric convergence 已缓解 Cohen's d 偏差；N3 因为论文已采用 binary F1 为主指标。
- **W2 (Ordinal vs nominal)** — 论文 Appendix H 已实验证明 ordinal classifier 无优势，framing 和 classifier choice 是独立决策。

[Round 1 审稿请求]:
你是 Reviewer 2，负责审查 **新颖性 + 写作质量 + 完整性**。

## 论文信息
- **标题**: "Depth Profiling: Classifying Recursive Generation Depth in Base LLM Text Chains"
- **路径**: `projects/gen-depth-fingerprint/artifacts/_project/paper/main.tex`
- **目标 venue**: EMNLP 2026 (ARR Long Paper)
- **截止日期**: 2026-05-25

## Venue 格式要求
- Long paper ≤ 8 页正文 + 无限参考文献 + 无限附录
- ACL 模板，`[review]` 选项
- Double-blind 匿名
- 必须包含 Limitations section（conclusion 之后、references 之前，不占页数）
- Overall Assessment 评分标准：5=Consider for Award, 4.5=Borderline Award, 4=Conference, 3.5=Borderline Conference, 3=Findings, 2.5=Borderline Findings, 2=Resubmit next cycle, 1.5=Resubmit after next cycle, 1=Do not resubmit

## 你的审查侧重
1. **新颖性评估**：Generation depth classification 这个任务定义是否有足够新颖性？DDR 框架的理论贡献在哪里？与已有 model collapse / detection 工作的差异是否实质性的？是否有被忽略的并行工作？
2. **写作质量**：
   - Abstract 是否在 30 秒内传达论文价值？
   - Introduction 叙事线是否连贯（问题→缺口→洞察→贡献）？
   - 全文主线是否统一（Introduction 的 framing 是否与 Discussion/Conclusion 一致）？
   - 是否有 AI 写作痕迹（em-dash、-ing phrases、过度分段、用词黑名单）？
   - 专有名词是否首次出现时有定义？
3. **完整性**：
   - Related Work 是否充分定位（不是 survey 式罗列）？引用密度是否足够？
   - Contribution 列表中每条是否有对应 section/table 支撑？
   - 附录内容是否在正文中有引用且融入叙事？
   - Limitations section 是否诚实全面？
4. **格式合规**：
   - 正文是否 ≤ 8 页？
   - 匿名化是否完整（无 author 泄漏）？
   - 图表字体 ≥ 7pt？Caption 自包含？
   - 引用格式一致？有无孤立 bib entry？

请阅读全文后，按以下结构输出审稿意见：
- Summary (2-3 句)
- Strengths (逐条)
- Weaknesses (逐条，标注 [Must-Fix] / [Should-Fix] / [Minor])
- Questions (需要作者回答的)
- Missing References
- Overall Assessment (评分 + 理由)
- Confidence (1-5)

_source line 2065 · 2026-05-19T03:20:51.252431+00:00_

# ARR Review Form Criteria and Scoring

Based on the provided guidelines, here is what the document specifies about review form dimensions:

## Primary Scoring Dimensions

**Soundness**
The guidelines indicate reviewers provide a "soundness" score that "must be justified by the text of the review." Low soundness scores require explicit justification in the review content.

**Excitement**
This represents a separate, more subjective dimension. The document notes: "you may find the work sound, but not something that is interesting for you personally. Such preferences are more subjective and should be reflected in a separate excitement score, which is orthogonal to the assessment of the soundness."

**Overall Assessment**
Described as "an explicit recommendation for the outcome of this paper, if it were committed to an *ACL venue." This composite score reflects soundness, excitement, novelty, and potential impact.

**Confidence**
The guidelines reference a confidence score in the ARR form, allowing reviewers to "indicate your confidence score."

**Reproducibility Score**
The document mentions low reproducibility scores "should be justified."

## Limitation

The document does not provide the exact numerical scales or complete descriptive anchors for these dimensions. It references "the ARR review form" and directs readers to a separate post on "[ARR review form details](https://aclrollingreview.org/reviewform)" for comprehensive scoring information.

_source line 2069 · 2026-05-19T03:21:11.188881+00:00_

# ACL Rolling Review Form - Scoring Dimensions

## Reviewer Confidence Scale
- **5**: "Positive that my evaluation is correct. I read the paper very carefully and am familiar with related work."
- **4**: "Quite sure. I tried to check the important points carefully."
- **3**: "Pretty sure, but there's a chance I missed something."
- **2**: "Willing to defend my evaluation, but it is fairly likely that I missed some details."
- **1**: "Not my area, or paper is very hard to understand."

## Soundness Scale
- **5**: "Excellent: This study is one of the most thorough I have seen, given its type."
- **4**: "Strong: This study provides sufficient support for all of its claims."
- **3**: "Acceptable: This study provides sufficient support for its main claims."
- **2**: "Poor: Some of the main claims are not sufficiently supported."
- **1**: "Major Issues: This study is not yet sufficiently thorough to warrant publication."

## Excitement Scale
- **5**: "Highly Exciting: I would recommend this paper to others and/or attend its presentation."
- **4**: "Exciting: I would mention this paper to others."
- **3**: "Interesting: I might mention some points of this paper to others."
- **2**: "Potentially Interesting: this paper does not resonate with me, but it might with others."
- **1**: "Not Exciting: this paper does not resonate with me."

## Overall Assessment Scale
- **5**: Consider for Award
- **4.5**: Borderline Award
- **4**: Conference acceptance
- **3.5**: Borderline Conference
- **3**: Findings acceptance
- **2.5**: Borderline Findings
- **2**: Resubmit next cycle
- **1.5**: Resubmit after next cycle
- **1**: Do not resubmit

1	\section{Introduction}
2	\label{sec:introduction}
3	
4	When LLM outputs are fed back as inputs to subsequent generation, the resulting recursive chains form a dynamical system whose distributional properties evolve with each step.
5	Characterizing the structure of this evolution is fundamental to understanding how language models transform distributions under iterative conditioning, with relevance to understanding model collapse dynamics \cite{curse_of_recursion, self_consuming_mad} and data provenance \cite{mapping_llm_science,monitoring_ai_review}.
6	Yet binary detection decisions \cite{fast_detectgpt,binoculars,ld_score,detectgpt} obscure this structure: a first-generation continuation retains much of the source distribution's lexical properties, while deeper recursive text exhibits measurably reduced vocabulary diversity and compressed perplexity.
7	
8	We formulate \emph{generation depth classification}, classifying text by generation depth (gen-0\,/\,gen-1\,/\,gen-2+) rather than a binary label, and term this \emph{depth profiling} (characterizing generative provenance from distributional statistics).
9	No existing method provides a feature-level decomposition of depth signal in non-paraphrastic continuation chains.
10	
11	The key finding is a \emph{differential depth response} (DDR) with a three-way partition: when we measure how eight base-model generators from five families (Pythia, OLMo, Qwen, Llama, Mistral; 0.6B--8B parameters) transform text across recursive depths, three \emph{drifting} features (reference-model mean entropy, type-token ratio, hapax ratio) shift monotonically (Cohen's $d > 0.75$), one \emph{intermediate} feature (entropy std) shows a non-monotonic level shift ($d{=}0.55$), and three \emph{near-stationary} features (entropy max, tail decay, entropy min) exhibit negligible depth effects ($d < 0.10$).
12	This is consistent with recursive generation shifting the NLL location while distributional shape remains stable under fixed sampling p

_source line 2156 · 2026-05-19T03:24:38.623444+00:00_

1	% Abstract — Depth Profiling of Recursive LLM Generation Chains
2	\begin{abstract}
3	Text generated by base (pretrained) large language models (LLMs) increasingly re-enters the web and seeds subsequent generation, and the resulting recursive chains progressively diverge from human-written text.
4	We ask whether this drift is structured enough to classify how many generation steps a text has undergone, framing this as \emph{generation depth classification} (gen-0\,/\,gen-1\,/\,gen-2+ rather than binary real-vs-synthetic).
5	Studying eight base (pretrained) models from five families (0.6B--8B parameters), we identify a \emph{differential depth response} (DDR): features partition into drifting (Cohen's $d > 0.75$), intermediate, and near-stationary ($d < 0.10$) groups under standard decoding ($t \leq 0.7$), consistent with recursive generation shifting the NLL location while distributional shape remains stable.
6	A 7-feature ensemble achieves leak-free binary F1\,=\,0.694 (95\% CI [.688, .700]) under leave-one-generator-out evaluation, generalizing across model families.
7	These results hold for base (pretrained) models; the depth signal degrades substantially under instruction tuning, restricting current applicability.
8	\end{abstract}
9

1	\section{Method}
2	\label{sec:method}
3	
4	\subsection{Task Formulation}
5	\label{sec:task}
6	
7	Given a text $x$, the goal is to predict its \emph{generation depth} $d \in \{0, 1, 2{+}\}$, where $d{=}0$ denotes human-written text, $d{=}1$ denotes first-generation LLM output conditioned on human text, and $d{=}2{+}$ denotes text produced by recursively conditioning an LLM on its own prior output at least twice.
8	Formally, let $\mathcal{C}_0$ be a reference corpus of human-written text.
9	A generation chain of depth $k$ is defined recursively: $x^{(0)} \sim \mathcal{C}_0$, and $x^{(k)} = G(x^{(k-1)})$ for $k \geq 1$, where $G$ denotes stochastic LLM generation conditioned on its input.
10	We frame depth estimation as an ordinal classification task over three classes rather than binary real-vs-synthetic detection, enabling finer-grained provenance characterization.
11	Depths beyond 2 are merged into a single $2{+}$ class, consistent with monotonic feature convergence that compresses inter-class margins beyond depth 2 (\S\ref{sec:results-differential}).
12	
13	\subsection{Generation Chain Construction}
14	\label{sec:generation}
15	
16	We construct generation chains using eight generators spanning five model families: \textbf{Pythia} (2.8B, 1B) \cite{pythia}, \textbf{OLMo} (OLMo-2-1B) \cite{olmo}, \textbf{Qwen} (Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-0.6B, Qwen3-8B-Base) \cite{qwen3}, \textbf{Llama} (Llama-3.2-3B) \cite{llama3}, and \textbf{Mistral} (Mistral-7B-v0.3) \cite{mistral7b}.
17	This selection covers parameter scales from 0.6B to 8B and diverse pretraining corpora, enabling evaluation of cross-generator and cross-scale generalization.
18	
19	For each generator, we sample approximately 1{,}000 seed passages from English Wikipedia as $\mathcal{C}_0$.
20	The same pool of seed passages serves as gen-0 for all generators.
21	We use shared seeds across generators so that cross-generator comparisons control for input content and isolate the effect of the generation model from source te
…[truncated 8112 chars]

1	\section{Results}
2	\label{sec:results}
3	
4	\subsection{Depth Classification Performance}
5	\label{sec:results-classification}
6	
7	The 7-feature ensemble achieves 3-class LOGO F1\,=\,0.762 (95\% CI [0.758, 0.766]; $N$\,=\,37{,}058) across eight generators from five families (Table~\ref{tab:logo-results}).
8	Because gen-0 seeds are shared across generators (\S\ref{sec:generation}), gen-0 classification is near-perfect (per-class F1\,$\approx$\,0.94) and the 3-class metric is inflated by this overlap.
9	We therefore adopt leak-free binary F1 (gen-1 vs.\ gen-2+)\,=\,0.694, CI [0.688, 0.700], as the primary metric throughout; it excludes gen-0 entirely and is unaffected by seed sharing.
10	Per-generator binary F1 ranges from 0.633 (Mistral-7B) to 0.724 (Pythia-2.8B), with the primary difficulty being gen-1 vs.\ gen-2+ discrimination (Appendix~\ref{app:confusion}).
11	
12	\paragraph{Cross-model generalization.}
13	Cross-model chains (randomly selected generator per step) yield binary F1\,=\,0.764 under 5-fold CV.\footnote{Cross-model chains use the original five-generator subset (Pythia, OLMo, Qwen); Llama and Mistral were added later for single-generator evaluation. Sample-level CV is used because each chain contains multiple generators, making generator-level held-out splits inapplicable.}
14	Leave-one-family-out (LOFO) evaluation yields binary F1\,=\,0.686, confirming cross-family transfer.
15	A scale effect emerges: Pythia generators achieve the highest binary F1 (0.718--0.724), while larger models show degradation (Mistral-7B: 0.633), partly reflecting same-family affinity with the Pythia-410M reference model (cross-reference spread $\leq$0.013; \S\ref{sec:results-differential}).
16	Preliminary cross-domain evaluation suggests the signal persists with variation (Appendix~\ref{app:domain}).
17	An end-to-end QLoRA classifier achieves 3-class F1\,=\,0.905 ($+$11.6\,pp; Appendix~\ref{app:qlora}), confirming that richer representations capture additional depth signal

1	\section{Discussion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	
4	The DDR tri-partition (3 drifting / 1 intermediate / 3 near-stationary) is consistent with a location/shape decomposition: under fixed temperature $\tau$ and nucleus $p$, the softmax output distribution's shape (variance, tail behavior) is constrained by the decoding configuration, while iterative conditioning shifts the input distribution's location as text becomes increasingly predictable to the reference model.
5	This interpretation is post-hoc and empirical; formal analysis of softmax output distribution dynamics under recursive conditioning remains an open problem.
6	DDR is an empirical descriptive framework capturing this dichotomy, not a claimed universal law of recursive generation.
7	DDR as characterized here is a parameter-dependent phenomenon (modulated by temperature and prompt type), not an intrinsic property of recursive generation independent of decoding conditions.
8	Temperature and prompt-type ablations (\S\ref{sec:results-differential}) support this as a tendency rather than a guarantee: all four tested generators degrade at $t{=}0.9$ ($-9.3$ to $-26.0$\,pp), and the signal varies across prompt types.
9	
10	Instruction tuning substantially weakens the signal across all three tested families (OLMo, Qwen, Llama; Appendix~\ref{app:instruct}).
11	Within-model 3-class F1 drops 3.7--19.9\,pp (OLMo: 0.575 vs.\ 0.754; Qwen: 0.732 vs.\ 0.769; Llama: 0.544 vs.\ 0.742), while binary F1 changes remain small ($\Delta \leq 3.2$\,pp), indicating that instruction tuning primarily disrupts the gen-0/gen-1 boundary.
12	Under cross-generator evaluation, the degradation is larger (OLMo-Instruct LOGO F1\,=\,0.338 vs.\ 0.763 base).
13	Entropy-mean $\rho$ reverses sign consistently ($+0.48$ for OLMo and Qwen, $+0.23$ for Llama; all base models ${\approx}{-}0.6$), and 6 of 7 features reverse direction for OLMo and Qwen, 3 of 7 for Llama, consistent with instruction tuning shifting the generation attractor rather than de

1	\section{Related Work}
2	\label{sec:related}
3	
4	\subsection{AI-Generated Text Detection}
5	\label{sec:related-detection}
6	
7	Methods for distinguishing human-written from machine-generated text have evolved from statistical tests \cite{gltr,grover,release_strategies} to zero-shot reference-model approaches \cite{detectgpt,fast_detectgpt,binoculars,dna_gpt} and learned classifiers that combine handcrafted features with neural architectures \cite{ghostbuster,feat_aug_transformer,abburi_multifaceted,reliable_detection,ld_score}.
8	Recent work pursues diverse signals including intrinsic dimensionality \cite{intrinsic_dimension}, surprisal diversity \cite{diveye}, and distributional gap measures \cite{generator_barrier}; see \citet{tang2024science}, \citet{wu_survey_2025}, and \citet{jawahar_survey} for surveys.
9	A persistent limitation is that these methods produce binary (real vs.\ synthetic) decisions.
10	Recent work moves beyond binary detection along several axes.
11	\citet{beyond_binary} classify text into four roles (human-only, LLM-polished, LLM-expanded, LLM-only) via involvement measurement, discriminating production \emph{modes} rather than recursive depth.
12	\citet{two_birds} jointly detect machine-generated text and attribute it to specific LLMs, and \citet{beyond_final_actor} distinguish dual creator-editor roles across four production modes.
13	Multi-generator attribution, including generator-level classification in SemEval 2024 Task 8 Subtask B \cite{semeval2024_task8} and the DeFactify dataset \cite{defactify_dataset}, discriminates among different \emph{sources}.
14	All these approaches classify text by its production process or authorship, while our depth classification tracks how many recursive generation steps a text has undergone, an orthogonal axis.
15	Even when detection scores correlate with depth, they exhibit high cross-generator variance as ordinal predictors (binary F1 from 0.52 to 0.71; Table~\ref{tab:baselines}), because each capture
…[truncated 5004 chars]

1	\section{Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:conclusion}
3	
4	We introduced generation depth classification and identified a differential depth response (DDR) tri-partition across eight base-model generators from five families.
5	A 7-feature ensemble exploiting this tri-partition achieves leak-free binary F1\,=\,0.694 (LOGO) and 0.764 (cross-model chains).
6	Current applicability is restricted to base models; instruction tuning and larger scales remain open.
7

1	\section*{Limitations}
2	\label{sec:limitations}
3	
4	All eight generators are base (pretrained) models.
5	This is a deliberate design choice: base models isolate the generation mechanism from instruction-tuning effects and enable controlled study of recursive distributional drift.
6	However, web text is predominantly produced by instruction-tuned and RLHF-aligned models.
7	Preliminary instruct experiments confirm that depth signal degrades substantially under instruction tuning: OLMo-2-1B-Instruct drops to LOGO F1\,=\,0.338 (vs.\ 0.763 base) and Qwen3-0.6B with chat template to 0.667 (vs.\ 0.816 base); these comparisons use earlier LOGO subsets (5--6 generators) predating the full 8-generator expansion in Table~\ref{tab:logo-results}.
8	Length-matched analysis yields near-zero correlations ($|\rho| < 0.02$) and binary classification near chance (F1\,$\approx$\,0.5) for Qwen models, leaving interpretation ambiguous.
9	Extending depth profiling to instruct models requires addressing these confounds (length regulation, format constraints, safety-driven distribution shaping) that instruction tuning introduces.
10	
11	Our experiments include generators up to 8.2B parameters (Qwen3-8B).
12	Depth signal persists at this scale (Qwen3-8B 3-class F1\,=\,0.714), but cross-scale transfer incurs a moderate gap vs.\ $\leq$2.8B generators (F1\,=\,0.77--0.81).
13	Frontier models (70B+) and closed-source models remain untested; the consistent scale trend (Mistral-7B binary F1\,=\,0.633, lowest among all generators) suggests depth signal may weaken further at frontier scales, potentially approaching the detection floor.
14	
15	Differential depth response strength is prompt-type dependent: continuation chains show strongest drift (within-type binary F1\,=\,0.781) compared to elaboration (0.730) and rewriting (0.737), with within-prompt-type paraphrastic chains showing weaker signal (binary F1\,=\,0.671 vs.\ 0.749 for within-prompt-type continuation).

1	\section*{Ethics Statement}
2	\label{sec:ethics}
3	
4	All texts analyzed in this work are LLM-generated from publicly available Wikipedia seeds; no personal or sensitive data is involved.
5	Our method estimates the generation depth of a text passage (how many recursive generation steps it has undergone), not the identity of its author.
6	Individual authorship attribution is outside both the scope and the capability of this approach; privacy implications are minimal.
7	We acknowledge that depth estimation tools could in principle be used to selectively suppress machine-generated text in contexts where such content is legitimate (e.g., accessibility tools, assistive writing).
8	We encourage deployment in data governance pipelines where provenance transparency, rather than content removal, is the objective.
9	All code, data, and trained models will be released upon publication.
10	
11	\paragraph{Licenses.}
12	All generator models are publicly available under permissive licenses (Apache 2.0 for Pythia, OLMo, and Mistral; Qwen License for Qwen3; Llama Community License for Llama 3.2).
13	Wikipedia seed passages are distributed under CC BY-SA 3.0.
14	
15	\paragraph{Compute budget.}
16	All experiments were conducted on 4$\times$ NVIDIA RTX 5090 GPUs (32\,GB each), totaling approximately 62 GPU-hours.
17	
18	\paragraph{Software.}
19	Experiments used Python 3.12.3, PyTorch 2.10.0 (CUDA 12.8), Transformers 4.57.6, XGBoost 3.2.0, scikit-learn 1.8.0, NumPy 2.2.6, pandas 3.0.2, and SciPy 1.17.1.
20

\section{Introduction}
\label{sec:introduction}

When LLM outputs are fed back as inputs to subsequent generation, the resulting recursive chains form a dynamical system whose distributional properties evolve with each step.
Characterizing the structure of this evolution is fundamental to understanding how language models transform distributions under iterative conditioning, with relevance to understanding model collapse dynamics \cite{curse_of_recursion, self_consuming_mad} and data provenance \cite{mapping_llm_science,monitoring_ai_review}.
Yet binary detection decisions \cite{fast_detectgpt,binoculars,ld_score,detectgpt} obscure this structure: a first-generation continuation retains much of the source distribution's lexical properties, while deeper recursive text exhibits measurably reduced vocabulary diversity and compressed perplexity.

We formulate \emph{generation depth classification}, classifying text by generation depth (gen-0\,/\,gen-1\,/\,gen-2+) rather than a binary label, and term this \emph{depth profiling} (characterizing generative provenance from distributional statistics).
No existing method provides a feature-level decomposition of depth signal in non-paraphrastic continuation chains.

The key finding is a \emph{differential depth response} (DDR) with a three-way partition: when we measure how eight base-model generators from five families (Pythia, OLMo, Qwen, Llama, Mistral; 0.6B--8B parameters) transform text across recursive depths, three \emph{drifting} features (reference-model mean entropy, type-token ratio, hapax ratio) shift monotonically (Cohen's $d > 0.75$), one \emph{intermediate} feature (entropy std) shows a non-monotonic level shift ($d{=}0.55$), and three \emph{near-stationary} features (entropy max, tail decay, entropy min) exhibit negligible depth effects ($d < 0.10$).
This is consistent with recursive generation shifting the NLL location while distributional shape remains stable under fixed sampling parameters.
While distributi
…[truncated 2082 chars]

?)\n        sentences = re.split(r'[.!?]+', para_clean)\n        sentences = [s.strip() for s in sentences if s.strip()]\n        \n        # Flag paragraphs with 1-2 sentences\n        if 1 <= len(sentences) <= 2:\n            # Double-check it's not a list or special structure\n            if not re.search(r'\\\\(item|begin|end|section|subsection|paragraph)', para):\n                short_paragraph_count += 1\n                short_paragraph_details.append({\n                    'file': tex_file,\n                    'paragraph_num': i,\n                    'sentences': len(sentences),\n                    'text_preview': para_clean[:80]\n                })\n\nprint(f\"Total short paragraphs (1-2 sentences, excluding structure): {short_paragraph_count}\\n\")\nif short_paragraph_details[:10]:\n    print(\"Sample short paragraphs:\")\n    for detail in short_paragraph_details[:10]:\n        print(f\"  {detail['file']} (para {detail['paragraph_num']}, {detail['sentences']} sent): {detail['text_preview']}...\")\n\nEOF\n"
```json
}
```

```json
  "command": "python3 << 'EOF'\nimport os\nimport re\n\npaper_dir = \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_3/projects/gen-depth-fingerprint/artifacts/_project/paper\"\n\n# Patterns to search for\npatterns = {\n    'We find': r'\\bWe find\\b',\n    'We show': r'\\bWe show\\b',\n    'We observe': r'\\bWe observe\\b',\n    'We provide': r'\\bWe provide\\b',\n    'We present': r'\\bWe present\\b',\n    'It is notable': r'\\bIt is notable\\b',\n    'These results': r'\\bThese results\\b',\n    'These findings': r'\\bThese findings\\b',\n}\n\nresults = {k: [] for k in patterns}\n\ntex_files = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(paper_dir) if f.endswith('.tex')])\n\nfor tex_file in tex_files:\n    filepath = os.path.join(paper_dir, tex_file)\n    try:\n        with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f:\n            lines = f.readlines()\n        \n        for i, line in enumerate(lines, 1):\n            for pattern_name, pattern_regex in patterns.items():\n                if re.search(pattern_regex, line):\n                    results[pattern_name].append((tex_file, i, line.strip()))\n    except:\n        pass\n\nprint(\"=== AI Writing Style Markers Found ===\\n\")\nfor pattern_name in sorted(patterns.keys()):\n    count = len(results[pattern_name])\n    if count > 0:\n        print(f\"{pattern_name}: {count} occurrences\")\n        for fname, line_num, text in results[pattern_name][:3]:\n            print(f\"  {fname}:{line_num} - {text[:70]}...\")\n        if count > 3:\n            print(f\"  ... and {count - 3} more\")\n        print()\n\nEOF\n"
}
```

```json
}
```

```json
  "command": "python3 << 'EOF'\nimport os\nimport re\n\npaper_dir = \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_3/projects/gen-depth-fingerprint/artifacts/_project/paper\"\n\n# Check the \"-ing,\" pattern more carefully\ntex_files = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(paper_dir) if f.endswith('.tex')])\n\nprint(\"=== PARTICIPIAL PHRASE SENTENCE-ENDINGS ('-ing,' or '-ing.')===\\n\")\n\ngenuine_participial = []\n\nfor tex_file in tex_files:\n    filepath = os.path.join(paper_dir, tex_file)\n    with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f:\n        lines = f.readlines()\n    \n    for i, line in enumerate(lines, 1):\n        # Look for pattern: word ending in -ing followed by punctuation\n        matches = re.finditer(r'(\\w+ing)[,\\.]', line)\n        for match in matches:\n            word = match.group(1)\n            context_start = max(0, match.start() - 50)\n            context_end = min(len(line), match.end() + 50)\n            context = line[context_start:context_end].strip()\n            \n            # Check if it's actually a participial phrase (not a noun like \"tuning\" in \"temperature tuning\")\n            # Participial phrases usually start mid-sentence after a noun/pronoun\n            preceding_text = line[:match.start()].strip()\n            \n            # Simple heuristic: if the -ing word could be an adjective/verb form describing an action\n            # typical participial examples: \"modeling,\", \"enabling,\", etc.\n            if any(x in word for x in ['ing']):\n                genuine_participial.append({\n                    'file': tex_file,\n                    'line': i,\n                    'word': word,\n                    'context': context[:80]\n                })\n\nprint(f\"Total -ing, / -ing. patterns: {len(genuine_participial)}\\n\")\n\n# Categorize them\nprint(\"By file:\")\nfile_counts = {}\nfor item in genuine_participial:\n    fname = item['file']\n    file_counts[fname] = file_counts.get(fname, 0) + 1\n\nfor fname in sorted(file_counts.keys()):\n    print(f\"  {fname}: {file_counts[fname]}\")\n\nprint(\"\\nDetailed breakdown:\")\nfor item in genuine_participial[:15]:\n    print(f\"  {item['file']}:{item['line']} - {item['word']}, in: {item['context']}...\")\n\nEOF\n"
}
```

```json
{
```
```json
  "text": "\n# Reviewer 2 审稿报告：Depth Profiling\n\n## Summary (2-3句)\n\n本文提出\"生成深度分类\"(generation depth classification)这一新任务，将文本按递归生成深度(gen-0/gen-1/gen-2+)进行分类，而非传统的二元真/合成检测。作者设计了7个手工特征并提出 DDR (Differential Depth Response) 三分法框架，在8个 base 模型上用 LOGO 交叉验证进行评估，binary F1=0.694、3-class F1=0.694。实验充分但方法论新颖性有限（特征工程+XGBoost），且实际应用价值尚未得到充分论证——论文自身实验显示 depth-aware curation 对下游 perplexity 无显著改善。\n\n## Strengths\n\n**S1. 任务定义有价值。** 将 AI 文本检测从二元分类扩展到多类深度分类，这一 framing 是新的且有意义的。它与 model collapse 文献的理论关注和数据溯源的实际需求都有自然联系。\n\n**S2. 实验设计严谨。** 8个生成器、5个模型家族、LOGO 交叉验证、Bonferroni 校正、TOST 等价检验——方法论上几乎无可挑剔。控制了 length confound（§4.1, Appendix B）、seed sharing（Appendix R）、prompt type（Appendix C）等多个混淆变量。\n\n**S3. 分析深度突出。** 20个附录（A-T）系统地覆盖了消融、混淆矩阵、特征分布、跨域迁移、跨温度迁移、扰动鲁棒性、下游效用等多个维度。DDR 的 drifting/intermediate/near-stationary 三分法（§4.2, Figure 1）提供了有用的分析视角。\n\n**S4. 写作质量良好。** 无 AI 写作痕迹（无 em-dash、无 -ing phrase 堆砌、无 discourse marker 滥用）。Abstract 信息密度高，行文简洁直接。引用完整（68条，无孤立/缺失），覆盖检测、collapse、数据质量、stylometry 等相关领域。\n\n**S5. 格式合规。** 正文≤8页（Conclusion 在第8页结束），Limitations 在 Conclusion 之后、References 之前，匿名化完整（\"Anonymous ACL submission\"），ACL 模板 review 选项。\n\n## Weaknesses\n\n### Must-Fix\n\n**N1 [critical] 实际应用动机薄弱——下游效用实验自我否定核心价值。**\n- 具体位置：Appendix L (Downstream Utility), Table 16, 以及 §5 Discussion\n- 问题：论文 Introduction（§1）将 depth classification 定位为理解 model collapse 和数据溯源的工具。但 Appendix L 的 TOST 等价检验表明 depth-aware curation 与 random subsampling 在下游 perplexity 上\"practically indistinguishable\"（Δ=0.046/0.770, p=1.1×10⁻¹）。这意味着**知道文本的生成深度对数据筛选没有实际帮助**。§8 Limitations 也承认 \"depth does not, by itself, make it more or less useful for training\"。\n- 为什么影响接收：如果深度分类对下游任务无可衡量的价值，论文的 motivation 从 \"this is useful\" 退化为 \"this is possible but we don't know why it matters\"。审稿人会问：\"So what?\"\n- 建议：(a) 找到一个 depth classification 确实改变决策/结果的下游应用场景（如检测 data contamination 中的多代累积漂移，或为 model collapse 研究提供精确的 depth labels）；(b) 或者诚实地重新定位论文为 analytical/empirical study，降低 utility claim。\n\n**N2 [critical] 仅限 base models，严重限制实际适用性。**\n- 具体位置：§3.2, §8 Limitations（第8-9页）\n- 问题：所有实验仅在 pretrained（base）模型上进行。但现实中绝大多数 LLM 生成文本来自 instruction-tuned 或 RLHF 模型。§4.2 和 Contribution 4 提到 \"instruction tuning reverses feature drift direction\"，但这是从 OLMo-Instruct 一个模型的简单观察（§8, 第8页），而非系统实验。\n- 为什么影响接收：如果方法在 instruction-tuned 模型上不工作（或表现完全不同），论文的实际价值大打折扣。审稿人期望至少有一个系统的 instruction-tuned 实验组。\n- 建议：增加至少 3-4 个 instruction-tuned 模型的实验（如 Llama-3-Instruct, Qwen-Chat, Mistral-Instruct），即使结果表明方法需要适配，这也是重要的 finding。\n\n**N3 [critical] DDR \"框架\"的理论贡献被过度包装。**\n- 具体位置：§4.2, Abstract (\"We identify a differential depth response (DDR)\")\n- 问题：DDR 本质上是将7个特征按其与 depth 的 Spearman 相关系数分为三组（|ρ|>0.60 = drifting, 0.10<|ρ|<0.55 = intermediate, |ρ|<0.10 = near-stationary）。这是描述性分析（descriptive categorization），不是一个\"框架\"（framework）。将相关性分组冠以 \"Differential Depth Response\" 的名称并作为核心贡献之一（Contribution 2），有过度包装之嫌。\n- 为什么影响接收：审稿人会质疑这是否构成方法论贡献。将特征按相关性排序是标准 EDA，不需要一个专有名词。\n- 建议：将 DDR 降级为分析发现而非框架贡献；或者为 DDR 提供理论基础（如为什么恰好是三组？是否有信息论解释？）。\n\n### Should-Fix\n\n**W1 [warning] 参考模型依赖（Pythia-410M）是未充分讨论的系统性混淆。**\n- 具体位置：§3.3 Feature Extraction, §7（第7页 \"reference model calibration is critical\"）\n- 问题：所有 reference-model features（entropy mean/std/min/max, tail decay）依赖于 Pythia-410M 作为参考模型。§7 承认 \"reference model calibration is critical for cross-domain deployment\"，但没有系统实验评估更换参考模型（如用 Qwen 或 Llama 作为参考）的影响。当生成器与参考模型属于同一家族（如 Pythia-2.8B 作为生成器）时，观测到的 depth signal 可能部分来自模型家族相似性而非真正的 depth effect。\n- 建议：(a) 增加 1-2 个备选参考模型的消融；(b) 分析 Pythia 家族生成器的 LOGO 性能是否系统性高于其他家族（从 Table 1 的数据看，Pythia-2.8B 确实是最高的 0.738）。\n\n**W2 [warning] 共享 seed passage 引入的统计依赖未充分处理。**\n- 具体位置：§3.2, Appendix R (Independent-Seed Control)\n- 问题：所有8个生成器共享同一组 ~1,000 个 Wikipedia seed passages。这意味着不同生成器的输出在 seed 维度上是 paired 的，违反了 independence 假设。Appendix R 用独立 seed 进行了对照实验（binary F1=0.693 vs 共享 seed 的 0.694），差距确实很小。但这个对照只用了6个生成器的 subset，且样本量（500 seeds × 5 depths = 2500 per generator）远小于主实验。\n- 建议：在主文中讨论 seed-sharing 的统计影响，并解释为什么 Appendix R 的小规模对照足以排除此混淆。\n\n**W3 [warning] 3-class 分组（gen-0 / gen-1 / gen-2+）的决策缺乏充分论证。**\n- 具体位置：§3.1 Task Formulation\n- 问题：§3.1 将 depth 2-4 合并为 \"gen-2+\" 类，理由是 \"monotonic feature convergence that compresses inter-class margins beyond depth 2\"。但这个决策在 Appendix D（Finer-Grained Depth Classification）的5-class 实验中被详细讨论，5-class F1 仅从 0.694 下降到约 0.50。论文没有清楚解释为什么不选择 4-class 或其他粒度。\n- 建议：在 §3.1 或 §4 中加入简要的 granularity 消融（如 2/3/4/5-class 的 F1 曲线），使 3-class 选择有数据支撑。\n\n**W4 [warning] Baseline 对比不完整。**\n- 具体位置：§4.3, Table 2\n- 问题：Table 2 的 baselines 是4个单特征检测分数（FastDetectGPT, Binoculars, AvgLogProb, FaithfulGPT）+ LD-Score，都不是为 depth classification 设计的。缺少：(a) 简单 n-gram 统计特征 baseline；(b) 除 QLoRA（Appendix E）外的其他 neural classifier（如 RoBERTa finetune）作为上界参考；(c) 直接使用 perplexity 作为 depth classifier 的 baseline。\n- 建议：增加至少一个 n-gram/简单统计 baseline 和一个预训练 encoder classifier baseline。\n\n### Minor\n\n**M1 [minor]** §1 Introduction 第一段提到 \"model collapse dynamics\" 和 \"data provenance\"，但这两个动机在后文（特别是 §5 Discussion）并未得到均等发展。Discussion 主要聚焦于 DDR 的描述性框架特征，对 model collapse 和 data provenance 的连接偏弱。\n\n**M2 [minor]** Table 1 caption 说 \"Binary F1 may carry upward bias from gen-0 seed sharing across generators\"，但 Appendix R 已经控制了这个问题。Caption 和正文对这一 limitation 的措辞不一致。\n\n**M3 [minor]** Figure 1（第6页）的 strip plot x 轴标签（hapas ratio, TTR 等）字体偏小，打印后可能低于 7pt。\n\n**M4 [minor]** §2.3 \"Vocabulary Diversity and Stylometry\" 小节引用密度偏低（4条引用），相比 §2.1 和 §2.2 明显稀疏。可补充 lexical diversity 方法的更多引用（如 MTLD、vocd-D 等已在 §3.3 使用但未在 Related Work 中讨论的工具）。\n\n**M5 [minor]** Appendix T (Illustrative Examples) 中 Table 24-25 展示了生成链示例及对应特征值，但只有1条链。增加 2-3 条不同生成器的链会更有说服力。\n\n## Questions\n\n**Q1.** Contribution 4 称 \"Depth composition does not determine downstream training utility\"——这是否意味着 depth classification 的主要应用场景不是数据筛选？如果是，作者认为最有价值的应用场景是什么？请给出具体用例。\n\n**Q2.** DDR 的三分法（drifting/intermediate/near-stationary）阈值（|ρ|=0.60, 0.10）是如何确定的？是否存在 sensitivity analysis？如果阈值改为 0.50 和 0.15，分组是否改变？\n\n**Q3.** 为什么不在 Related Work 中讨论 iterative self-training / self-play 文献（如 Constitutional AI、SPIN 等）？这些方法也涉及多代文本生成，且其输出也可能出现在训练数据中。\n\n**Q4.** Pythia-410M 作为参考模型时，Pythia 家族生成器（Pythia-1B, Pythia-2.8B）是否因为架构相似性而获得系统性优势？Table 1 显示 Pythia-2.8B 的 binary F1 最高（0.738），这是否支持这一假设？\n\n**Q5.** 论文提到 instruction tuning \"reverses feature drift direction\"（§4.2, §8）。这个观察基于多少个 instruction-tuned 模型？如果只有 OLMo-Instruct 一个，这个 claim 的可靠性如何？\n\n## Missing References\n\n1. **Self-training/self-play 文献**：如 Yuan et al., 2024 \"Self-Rewarding Language Models\"; Chen et al., 2024 \"Self-Play Fine-Tuning\" (SPIN)——这些涉及多代生成且与 depth profiling 直接相关。\n2. **Data attribution 方法**：如 Park et al., 2023 \"TRAK: Attributing Model Behavior at Scale\"——与数据溯源动机直接相关。\n3. **更多 lexical diversity 方法论文**：MTLD (McCarthy & Jarvis, 2010) 和 MATTR (Covington & McFall, 2010) 已在特征设计中使用但未在 §2.3 Related Work 中讨论。\n\n## 官方维度打分（ACL ARR Review Form）\n\n| 维度 | 分数 | 理由摘要 |\n|------|------|----------|\n| Soundness | 3/5 | 实验设计严谨、统计检验充分，但核心 claim（DDR 作为\"框架\"）的支撑偏弱；下游效用实验否定了实际应用动机；参考模型混淆未系统评估 |\n| Excitement | 2/5 | 任务定义有新意，但方法论（特征工程+XGBoost）缺乏 methodological novelty；仅限 base models 限制了实际影响力；未能论证 depth classification 的 killer application |\n| Reproducibility | 4/5 | 代码/数据承诺公开，实验细节充分（模型、超参数、评估协议均有明确说明），但参考模型选择的影响未知 |\n| Overall Assessment | 3.0 | Findings 水平 |\n| Confidence | 4/5 | 熟悉 AI 文本检测和 model collapse 文献，仔细阅读了全文和全部附录 |\n\n## Overall Assessment\n\n**Overall**: 3.0 (Findings)\n**一句话总结**: 任务定义有价值且实验充分，但方法论新颖性有限（标准特征+标准分类器），且论文自身实验否定了最直觉的应用场景（数据筛选），使得 \"为什么要做 depth classification\" 这个问题缺乏令人信服的回答。\n\n论文最大的优势是实验的系统性和分析深度（20个附录覆盖了几乎所有潜在质疑）。最大的弱点是缺乏一个 compelling 的应用场景来证明 depth classification 的价值超越 binary detection。如果作者能 (1) 在 instruction-tuned 模型上验证方法，(2) 找到一个 depth classification 确实改变决策的应用场景，(3) 适当降低 DDR \"框架\"的包装力度，本文有潜力达到 3.5 (Borderline Conference) 水平。\n\n**Confidence**: 4/5 — 熟悉相关文献，全文细读。\n",

模型上不工作（或表现完全不同），论文的实际价值大打折扣。审稿人期望至少有一个系统的 instruction-tuned 实验组。\n- 建议：增加至少 3-4 个 instruction-tuned 模型的实验（如 Llama-3-Instruct, Qwen-Chat, Mistral-Instruct），即使结果表明方法需要适配，这也是重要的 finding。\n\n**N3 [critical] DDR \"框架\"的理论贡献被过度包装。**\n- 具体位置：§4.2, Abstract (\"We identify a differential depth response (DDR)\")\n- 问题：DDR 本质上是将7个特征按其与 depth 的 Spearman 相关系数分为三组（|ρ|>0.60 = drifting, 0.10<|ρ|<0.55 = intermediate, |ρ|<0.10 = near-stationary）。这是描述性分析（descriptive categorization），不是一个\"框架\"（framework）。将相关性分组冠以 \"Differential Depth Response\" 的名称并作为核心贡献之一（Contribution 2），有过度包装之嫌。\n- 为什么影响接收：审稿人会质疑这是否构成方法论贡献。将特征按相关性排序是标准 EDA，不需要一个专有名词。\n- 建议：将 DDR 降级为分析发现而非框架贡献；或者为 DDR 提供理论基础（如为什么恰好是三组？是否有信息论解释？）。\n\n### Should-Fix\n\n**W1 [warning] 参考模型依赖（Pythia-410M）是未充分讨论的系统性混淆。**\n- 具体位置：§3.3 Feature Extraction, §7（第7页 \"reference model calibration is critical\"）\n- 问题：所有 reference-model features（entropy mean/std/min/max, tail decay）依赖于 Pythia-410M 作为参考模型。§7 承认 \"reference model calibration is critical for cross-domain deployment\"，但没有系统实验评估更换参考模型（如用 Qwen 或 Llama 作为参考）的影响。当生成器与参考模型属于同一家族（如 Pythia-2.8B 作为生成器）时，观测到的 depth signal 可能部分来自模型家族相似性而非真正的 depth effect。\n- 建议：(a) 增加 1-2 个备选参考模型的消融；(b) 分析 Pythia 家族生成器的 LOGO 性能是否系统性高于其他家族（从 Table 1 的数据看，Pythia-2.8B 确实是最高的 0.738）。\n\n**W2 [warning] 共享 seed passage 引入的统计依赖未充分处理。**\n- 具体位置：§3.2, Appendix R (Independent-Seed Control)\n- 问题：所有8个生成器共享同一组 ~1,000 个 Wikipedia seed passages。这意味着不同生成器的输出在 seed 维度上是 paired 的，违反了 independence 假设。Appendix R 用独立 seed 进行了对照实验（binary F1=0.693 vs 共享 seed 的 0.694），差距确实很小。但这个对照只用了6个生成器的 subset，且样本量（500 seeds × 5 depths = 2500 per generator）远小于主实验。\n- 建议：在主文中讨论 seed-sharing 的统计影响，并解释为什么 Appendix R 的小规模对照足以排除此混淆。\n\n**W3 [warning] 3-class 分组（gen-0 / gen-1 / gen-2+）的决策缺乏充分论证。**\n- 具体位置：§3.1 Task Formulation\n- 问题：§3.1 将 depth 2-4 合并为 \"gen-2+\" 类，理由是 \"monotonic feature convergence that compresses inter-class margins beyond depth 2\"。但这个决策在 Appendix D（Finer-Grained Depth Classification）的5-class 实验中被详细讨论，5-class F1 仅从 0.694 下降到约 0.50。论文没有清楚解释为什么不选择 4-class 或其他粒度。\n- 建议：在 §3.1 或 §4 中加入简要的 granularity 消融（如 2/3/4/5-class 的 F1 曲线），使 3-class 选择有数据支撑。\n\n**W4 [warning] Baseline 对比不完整。**\n- 具体位置：§4.3, Table 2\n- 问题：Table 2 的 baselines 是4个单特征检测分数（FastDetectGPT, Binoculars, AvgLogProb, FaithfulGPT）+ LD-Score，都不是为 depth classification 设计的。缺少：(a) 简单 n-gram 统计特征 baseline；(b) 除 QLoRA（Appendix E）外的其他 neural classifier（如 RoBERTa finetune）作为上界参考；(c) 直接使用 perplexity 作为 depth classifier 的 baseline。\n- 建议：增加至少一个 n-gram/简单统计 baseline 和一个预训练 encoder classifier baseline。\n\n### Minor\n\n**M1 [minor]** §1 Introduction 第一段提到 \"model collapse dynamics\" 和 \"data provenance\"，但这两个动机在后文（特别是 §5 Discussion）并未得到均等发展。Discussion 主要聚焦于 DDR 的描述性框架特征，对 model collapse 和 data provenance 的连接偏弱。\n\n**M2 [minor]** Table 1 caption 说 \"Binary F1 may carry upward bias from gen-0 seed sharing across generators\"，但 Appendix R 已经控制了这个问题。Caption 和正文对这一 limitation 的措辞不一致。\n\n**M3 [minor]** Figure 1（第6页）的 strip plot x 轴标签（hapas ratio, TTR 等）字体偏小，打印后可能低于 7pt。\n\n**M4 [minor]** §2.3 \"Vocabulary Diversity and Stylometry\" 小节引用密度偏低（4条引用），相比 §2.1 和 §2.2 明显稀疏。可补充 lexical diversity 方法的更多引用（如 MTLD、vocd-D 等已在 §3.3 使用但未在 Related Work 中讨论的工具）。\n\n**M5 [minor]** Appendix T (Illustrative Examples) 中 Table 24-25 展示了生成链示例及对应特征值，但只有1条链。增加 2-3 条不同生成器的链会更有说服力。\n\n## Questions\n\n**Q1.** Contribution 4 称 \"Depth composition does not determine downstream training utility\"——这是否意味着 depth classification 的主要应用场景不是数据筛选？如果是，作者认为最有价值的应用场景是什么？请给出具体用例。\n\n**Q2.** DDR 的三分法（drifting/intermediate/near-stationary）阈值（|ρ|=0.60, 0.10）是如何确定的？是否存在 sensitivity analysis？如果阈值改为 0.50 和 0.15，分组是否改变？\n\n**Q3.** 为什么不在 Related Work 中讨论 iterative self-training / self-play 文献（如 Constitutional AI、SPIN 等）？这些方法也涉及多代文本生成，且其输出也可能出现在训练数据中。\n\n**Q4.** Pythia-410M 作为参考模型时，Pythia 家族生成器（Pythia-1B, Pythia-2.8B）是否因为架构相似性而获得系统性优势？Table 1 显示 Pythia-2.8B 的 binary F1 最高（0.738），这是否支持这一假设？\n\n**Q5.** 论文提到 instruction tuning \"reverses feature drift direction\"（§4.2, §8）。这个观察基于多少个 instruction-tuned 模型？如果只有 OLMo-Instruct 一个，这个 claim 的可靠性如何？\n\n## Missing References\n\n1. **Self-training/self-play 文献**：如 Yuan et al., 2024 \"Self-Rewarding Language Models\"; Chen et al., 2024 \"Self-Play Fine-Tuning\" (SPIN)——这些涉及多代生成且与 depth profiling 直接相关。\n2. **Data attribution 方法**：如 Park et al., 2023 \"TRAK: Attributing Model Behavior at Scale\"——与数据溯源动机直接相关。\n3. **更多 lexical diversity 方法论文**：MTLD (McCarthy & Jarvis, 2010) 和 MATTR (Covington & McFall, 2010) 已在特征设计中使用但未在 §2.3 Related Work 中讨论。\n\n## 官方维度打分（ACL ARR Review Form）\n\n| 维度 | 分数 | 理由摘要 |\n|------|------|----------|\n| Soundness | 3/5 | 实验设计严谨、统计检验充分，但核心 claim（DDR 作为\"框架\"）的支撑偏弱；下游效用实验否定了实际应用动机；参考模型混淆未系统评估 |\n| Excitement | 2/5 | 任务定义有新意，但方法论（特征工程+XGBoost）缺乏 methodological novelty；仅限 base models 限制了实际影响力；未能论证 depth classification 的 killer application |\n| Reproducibility | 4/5 | 代码/数据承诺公开，实验细节充分（模型、超参数、评估协议均有明确说明），但参考模型选择的影响未知 |\n| Overall Assessment | 3.0 | Findings 水平 |\n| Confidence | 4/5 | 熟悉 AI 文本检测和 model collapse 文献，仔细阅读了全文和全部附录 |\n\n## Overall Assessment\n\n**Overall**: 3.0 (Findings)\n**一句话总结**: 任务定义有价值且实验充分，但方法论新颖性有限（标准特征+标准分类器），且论文自身实验否定了最直觉的应用场景（数据筛选），使得 \"为什么要做 depth classification\" 这个问题缺乏令人信服的回答。\n\n论文最大的优势是实验的系统性和分析深度（20个附录覆盖了几乎所有潜在质疑）。最大的弱点是缺乏一个 compelling 的应用场景来证明 depth classification 的价值超越 binary detection。如果作者能 (1) 在 instruction-tuned 模型上验证方法，(2) 找到一个 depth classification 确实改变决策的应用场景，(3) 适当降低 DDR \"框架\"的包装力度，本文有潜力达到 3.5 (Borderline Conference) 水平。\n\n**Confidence**: 4/5 — 熟悉相关文献，全文细读。\n",
    "reviewer_id": "2",
    "project": "gen-depth-fingerprint"
  }
```
```json
}
```

以下是 Reviewer 1 的完整审稿意见，请逐条回应：对每个 Weakness/Question 标注 [AGREE] 同意并说明理由，或 [REBUT] 反驳并提供证据（cite 具体 Section/Table/Figure）。

# Reviewer 1 审稿报告

## Summary
本文提出 generation depth classification 任务，将文本分类为 gen-0/gen-1/gen-2+，并在 8 个 base model（5 个 family，0.6B–8B）上识别出 DDR 三分结构：3 个 drifting 特征、1 个 intermediate 特征、3 个 near-stationary 特征。7 特征集成在 LOGO 评估下获得 leak-free binary F1 = 0.694。

## Must-Fix

**N1 [critical] Gen-0 seed sharing 使核心三分类评估失效。**
- gen-0 seeds 在所有 generators 间共享，LOGO 中 gen-0 文本训练/测试完全相同。Confusion matrix 显示 gen-0 recall=99.4%。Independent-seed 实验仅 2 generators。论文 framing（三分类 depth profiling）与实际可靠评估（仅 binary）之间存在显著落差。

**N2 [critical] 仅限 base model 的限制对论文 significance 造成根本性约束。**
- Appendix K 显示 instruct 模型下 entropy mean ρ 翻转（−0.60→+0.48），6/7 特征方向反转，LOGO F1 从 0.763 暴跌至 0.338。DDR 核心结构在 instruct model 下被根本性颠覆。论文多处暗示更广泛适用性，超出实验支撑。

## Should-Fix

**W1 [warning] Tail decay "near-stationary" 分类缺乏充分统计支撑。**
- TOST 仅 4/8 generators 通过，non-passing 的 |d| 范围 0.15–0.22。需放松至 ε=0.25 才达 7/8。

**W2 [warning] Downstream TOST n=3 seeds 统计效力极低。**
- Cohen's d 值高达 86.0/119.7（artificial inflation），以 n=3 支撑跨 Section 反复引用的结论，统计基础过于薄弱。

**W3 [warning] 4-detector ensemble baseline 反超削弱 DDR 方法论贡献。**
- 4-det ensemble binary F1=0.708 > 7-feature 0.705。现有检测方法已隐含深度信息，简单组合就能达到或超过 DDR。

**W4 [warning] 多个 generator subset 混用。**
- 5/6/8-gen subsets，cross-reference 难以直接对比 main results。

**W5 [warning] Cross-domain 实验 t=1.0 超出可靠范围。**
- 论文自身确认 t≥0.9 退化 9.3-26.0pp，domain 和 temperature 效应混淆。

**W6 [warning] Cohen's d 异方差问题。**
- Levene tests 拒绝等方差（p<10^-10），但正文仍用 pooled Cohen's d 定义关键阈值。

## Minor
- M1: "base (pretrained)" 重复注释冗余
- M2: Table 1 脚注关键信息过于不显眼
- M3: 3 个 drifting features 高度相关（r=0.81-0.86），可能是同一 latent factor
- M4: Qualitative example 仅 Pythia，退化过于极端
- M5: Ethics Statement 缺 CO₂ 排放估算
- M6: Discussion 中 "gen-1 per-class F1=0.63" 指代不明

## Questions
- Q1: 能否在 full 8-gen LOGO 下报告 independent-seed 3-class F1？
- Q2: 8-gen 设置下 cross-reference spread 是否仍 ≤0.013？
- Q3: 是否考虑 PCA/factor analysis 验证 drifting group 是否为单一 latent factor？
- Q4: Cross-prompt transfer 在多 generator 设置下是否稳健？
- Q5: Qwen3-1.7B t=0.5/0.7 binary F1 完全相同（0.751），CI 缺失原因？
- Q6: Table 2 pooled vs per-generator macro average 差异从何而来？

## Missing References
- Bao et al. (2024) "On the Reliability of AI Text Detectors"
- 更多 ordinal regression NLP 文献
- Guo et al. (2023) "How Close is ChatGPT to Human Experts?"

## Overall: 3.0 (Findings), Confidence: 4/5

called mcp__agent-ml-research__review_reviewer_post_to_director

<details open><summary>🔧 Tool call — mcp__agent-ml-research__review_reviewer_post_to_director</summary>

```json
{
  "text": "\n# Reviewer 2 对 Reviewer 1 的交叉回应\n\n---\n\n## Must-Fix\n\n**[AGREE] R1-N1: Gen-0 seed sharing 使三分类评估存在 leakage 风险。**\n\nR1 的核心洞察正确：LOGO 中 held-out generator 的 gen-0 文本与训练集 gen-0 完全相同（同一批 Wikipedia passages），构成 data leakage。Confusion matrix（Figure 5b, p.22）显示 gen-0 recall=99.4%，远高于 gen-1 的 75.0% 和 gen-2+ 的 73.7%，这个不对称性与 leakage 假设一致。\n\nAppendix R（p.28）的独立 seed 对照实验确实缓解了这一问题（binary F1: 0.693 vs. 0.694，差异 <0.001），但有两个重要局限：(1) 仅用了 Pythia-2.8B 和 OLMo-2-1B 两个生成器；(2) 未报告 3-class F1。这正是 R1 Q1 所要求的。\n\n**补充 R1 分析**：不过，我认为此问题的严重程度略低于 R1 的判断。3-class macro F1 对三个类别等权平均，gen-0 recall 从 99.4% 降至（假设无 leakage 下的）~90% 仅导致 macro F1 下降 ~3pp（从 0.694 → ~0.66）。真正的 classification 挑战在 gen-1 vs gen-2+ 边界（recall 分别为 75.0% 和 73.7%），这部分不受 gen-0 seed sharing 影响。因此这更接近 should-fix 而非 critical，但作者确实需要补充 full 8-gen independent-seed 3-class F1。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R1-N2: 仅限 base model 对论文 significance 构成根本性约束。**\n\n完全同意。这也是我的 N2。Appendix K（p.16, Table 10）的数据具有破坏性：OLMo-Instruct LOGO F1 从 base 的 0.763 暴跌至 0.338，entropy mean ρ 从 −0.60 翻转至 +0.48，6/7 特征方向反转。这意味着 DDR 的 drifting/intermediate/near-stationary 三分结构在 instruct 模型下可能完全失效。\n\n§8 Limitations（p.8）承认 \"instruction tuning substantially weakens the signal across all three tested families\"，但论文多处措辞（如 §1 \"how many recursive generation steps a text has undergone\"、§6 Conclusion \"classifying text by generation depth\"）暗示了超出 base-model 范围的一般性。作者应当：(a) 在 title/abstract 中明确限定 \"base LLM\" 范围；(b) 至少在 3-4 个 instruct 模型上报告系统实验结果（即使结果为负面）。\n\n---\n\n## Should-Fix\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W1: Tail decay \"near-stationary\" 分类缺乏充分统计支撑。**\n\n§4.2（p.6）明确承认 tail decay 的 TOST 仅 4/8 generators 通过。R1 指出需将 ε 放松至 0.25 才达 7/8，这进一步削弱了 \"near-stationary\" 标签。从 §4.2 的数据看，tail decay 的 |d| 范围 0.15–0.22 已接近论文自定义的 intermediate 区间（d=0.16 在 §4.2 被标记为 \"making it the sole intermediate feature\"）。\n\n建议将 tail decay 从 \"near-stationary\" 重新归类为 \"intermediate\"，或提供更严格的 per-generator 证据。这也会影响 DDR 三分法的对称性（从 3/1/3 变为 3/2/2），但这是更诚实的分类。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W2: Downstream TOST n=3 统计效力极低。**\n\n同意。n=3 下 Cohen's d=86.0/119.7 显然是 artificial inflation（pooled SD 极小导致 d 值爆炸）。论文在 p.20 承认 \"The inflated effect sizes reflect low seed-level variance (confirming reproducibility) rather than practically meaningful perplexity differences\"，但仍在 §5 Discussion（p.7-8）和 Contribution 4 中将此结论作为核心贡献引用。\n\n这与我的 N1 从不同角度指向同一问题：downstream utility 实验既统计效力不足（R1 的 W2），结果本身又否定了 depth classification 的实际价值（我的 N1）。两者叠加使 Contribution 4 (\"Depth composition does not determine downstream training utility\") 成为全文最薄弱的 claim。\n\n---\n\n**[REBUT] R1-W3: 4-detector ensemble baseline 反超的严重性被夸大。**\n\nR1 的数据引用有偏差。从 Table 2（p.7）看：\n- Binary F1: 4-det ensemble = 0.706, 7-feature = 0.705（Δ=0.001，在 noise 范围内）\n- 3-class F1: 4-det ensemble = 0.785, 7-feature = 0.714（Δ=0.071，确实显著）\n\n但 §4.3 已明确声明定位：\"To contextualize depth discrimination ability rather than claim competitive advantage over detection-optimized methods.\" 论文未声称 7-feature 方法在性能上优于现有检测器，而是强调其可解释性优势——DDR 框架提供了 feature-level decomposition（哪些信号在 drift、哪些 stationary），而 detection scores 是黑盒。\n\n**我部分同意 R1 的隐含结论**：existing detection scores 也能隐含地编码 depth 信息，这确实削弱了 DDR 的 unique methodological contribution。但 R1 将 binary F1 差异 0.001 称为\"反超\"是不准确的。实际上 7-feature 和 4-det ensemble 在 binary 上 performance-equivalent，而 4-det 在 3-class 上更优。作者应在 §4.3 中更诚实地讨论这一点。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W4: 多个 generator subset 混用造成混乱。**\n\n论文在不同 section/appendix 使用了 5-gen（§3.2, Table 1 footnote 指出原始 6-gen subset）、6-gen（Appendix G/H/I 等）和 8-gen（Table 1 main results）三个 subset，且没有一个统一的对照表说明何时用何 subset 及原因。\n\nTable 1 footnote 指出 \"Qwen3-1.7B(0.6B) added later\"，但具体哪些实验使用哪个 subset 需要在正文中有清晰的一处说明。这是 presentation 问题，不影响实验有效性，但确实增加了审稿人的认知负担。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W5: Cross-domain 实验 t=1.0 与 main experiment t=0.7 不一致。**\n\nAppendix J（p.18）的 cross-domain 实验使用 t=1.0，而 main experiment（§3.2）使用 t=0.7。Appendix H（p.16, Table 10）显示 t=1.0 vs t=0.7 可导致 F1 差异 ~4-7pp。更关键的是，Appendix J 自身数据显示 \"at t=0.7, Academic-domain binary F1 drops to 0.626\"（p.18），比 t=1.0 下的 0.739 低了 11.3pp。\n\n这意味着论文报告的 cross-domain robustness（F1>0.73）部分归功于 t=1.0 的高温度而非真正的 domain 迁移能力。在 t=0.7 下 Academic domain 降至 0.626，这实际上削弱了 cross-domain 的 claim。R1 的混淆效应指控成立。\n\n---\n\n**[REBUT] R1-W6: Cohen's d 异方差问题已在论文中被处理。**\n\n§4.2（p.6）明确说明 \"This partition is robust under heteroscedasticity-corrected metrics (Glass's Δ, Cliff's δ; Appendix V)\"。论文使用 pooled Cohen's d 作为 primary presentation metric（因其更直观），但 DDR 分区在 Glass's Δ 和 Cliff's δ 下保持不变。\n\nR1 的技术观察正确（Levene test 确实拒绝等方差），但结论性 claim 不成立——论文已检验了异方差稳健替代指标并确认结果一致。这是一个 minor presentation issue（应在正文中更显著地提及 Glass's Δ 结果），不是 should-fix。\n\n---\n\n## Minor\n\n**[REBUT] R1-M1: \"base (pretrained)\" 重复注释**\n学术论文中 \"base model\" 的含义不固定（有些领域指 backbone/foundation model）。\"base (pretrained)\" 在首次使用时提供明确定义是良好写作实践，符合论文写作规范 §1.3 \"首次出现的专有名词必须给出完整定义\"。后续使用中仅用 \"base\" 即可，无需每次重复。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-M2: Table 1 脚注信息过于不显眼。**\nTable 1 脚注 \"Binary F1 may carry upward bias from gen-0 seed sharing\" 涉及核心方法论关切（与 N1 直接相关），不应藏在脚注中。建议提升至正文讨论。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-M3: 3 drifting features 高度相关可能是单一 latent factor。**\nAppendix Q（p.27）证实 TTR 和 hapax ratio 的 pairwise correlation r ≈ 0.97，且两者与 entropy mean 的 r ≈ 0.56-0.81。R1 提出的 PCA/factor analysis 建议（Q3）有价值——如果3个 drifting features 确实是一个 latent factor，DDR 的 \"3 drifting + 1 intermediate + 3 near-stationary\" 结构在维度上被高估了。论文对此有初步讨论（§4.2 \"sharing a single latent dimension\"），但未做正式验证。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-M4: Qualitative example 仅 Pythia，退化过于极端。**\nAppendix T（p.29）的 Table 24 仅展示了一条 Pythia-2.8B 链，且 gen-4 文本退化到近乎无意义的重复。增加来自 Qwen 或 Llama 的链（可能退化更平缓）会提供更有代表性的示例。\n\n**[REBUT] R1-M5: Ethics Statement 缺 CO₂ 排放估算。**\nCO₂ 排放估算在 EMNLP/ARR 中非强制要求。论文已报告 compute budget（p.9：\"4× NVIDIA RTX 5090 GPUs (32GB each), totaling approximately 62 GPU-hours\"），这已满足 reproducibility 需求。CO₂ 可在 camera-ready 中补充，不构成审稿问题。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-M6: \"gen-1 per-class F1=0.63\" 指代不明。**\n§5 Discussion 中引用的 0.63 应标注来源（是 Table 1 的哪个指标？是 pooled 还是 per-generator macro？是 binary 还是 3-class per-class？）。缺乏精确引用降低了叙述可信度。\n\n---\n\n## Questions 回应\n\n**Q1: Full 8-gen independent-seed 3-class F1** — [AGREE] 这是对 N1 最直接的回应，应由作者提供。如果 3-class F1 在 independent-seed 下仍接近 analysis 验证 drifting group** — [AGREE] 这与 M3 直接相关。TTR/hapax ratio 的 r=0.97（Appendix Q）强烈暗示它们是同一 latent factor 的两个 proxy。正式的 factor analysis 会决定 DDR 的 3-drifting 结构是否成立还是应合并为 1-2 个 factor。\n\n**Q4: p.16 Table 10 中确认 Qwen3-1.7B 的两行（t=0.5 和 t=0.7）确实有相同的 binary F1=0.751。虽然可能是数值巧合（bootstrap CI 可能不同但四舍五入后一致），但作者应报告 CI 或至少解释。\n\n**Q6: Table 2 pooled vs per-generator macro average 差异** — [AGREE] Table 2 caption 提到 \"slightly higher than per-generator macro average in Table 1\"，但没解释差异来源。Pooled evaluation 和 per-generator macro average 在 class imbalance 下可以不同（pooled 对大 generator 加权更多）。作者应明确说明。\n\n---\n\n## Missing References 回应\n\n**Bao et al. [Round 1 审稿请求]:
```
你是 Reviewer 2，负责审查论文的 **新颖性、完整性、写作质量与格式合规**。

## 论文信息
- **标题**: Depth Profiling: Classifying Recursive Generation Depth in Base LLM Text Chains
- **路径**: `projects/gen-depth-fingerprint/artifacts/_project/paper/main.tex`（PDF: `main.pdf`）
- **目标 venue**: EMNLP 2026 via ACL Rolling Review (Long Paper)
- **截止日期**: 2026-05-25
- **格式要求**: 正文 ≤ 8 页，ACL 模板 `[review]`，double-blind，Limitations 必须有，附录不要求审阅但建议检查
- **ARR 评分标准**: 5=Consider for Award, 4.5=Borderline Award, 4=Conference, 3.5=Borderline Conference, 3=Findings, 2.5=Borderline Findings, 2=Resubmit next cycle, 1.5=Resubmit after next cycle, 1=Do not resubmit

## 你的审查侧重

**重点审查以下方面**：

1. **新颖性与贡献**:
   - "Generation depth classification" 作为新任务的价值——是否真正填补了 gap，还是 niche 问题？
   - DDR tri-partition 作为 "empirical rather than methodological" 贡献的充分性——对 EMNLP 而言是否有足够的 insight？
   - 与两篇 concurrent works (Broken Telephone, Markovian Chains) 的区分是否有说服力？
   - 限制在 base models 对 significance 的影响——web text 主要由 instruct models 产生

2. **Related Work 覆盖与定位**:
   - 引用是否充分（当前 ~40 篇）？
   - 是否有重要遗漏（特别是 ordinal classification、data contamination detection、self-training 领域）？
   - Positioning 是否清晰——本文与 AI text detection、model collapse、stylometry 三个领域的关系

3. **写作质量**:
   - Abstract/Introduction 的故事线是否连贯有吸引力？
   - 专有名词首次出现是否有定义？
   - 是否存在 AI 写作风格标志（em-dash、-ing phrases、过度分段、禁用词）？
   - 段落/句式是否自然流畅？
   - 主线统一性：Abstract → Introduction → Method → Results → Discussion → Conclusion 是否一致？

4. **格式合规**:
   - 正文是否 ≤ 8 页？
   - 模板是否正确（`\usepackage[review]{acl}`）？
   - 匿名化是否到位（无作者信息泄露）？
   - 图表字体是否 ≥ 7pt？信息密度是否足够？
   - Limitations section 是否充分？
   - Ethics Statement 是否充分？

5. **叙事结构**:
   - Contribution list 是否每条都有对应的 section/evidence 支撑？
   - 附录内容是否在正文中有引用且融入叙事？
   - 数据是否服务于叙事推进（vs 堆砌）？
   - Conclusion 是否与 Introduction 的 promise 对应？

## 输出格式

请按以下结构输出审稿意见：
1. **Summary** (2-3 句)
2. **Strengths** (逐条)
3. **Weaknesses** (逐条，标注严重程度: [Critical] / [Major] / [Minor])
4. **Questions** (需要作者回答的)
5. **Missing References** (如有)
6. **Overall Assessment** (使用 ARR 评分: 1-5 scale，参照上述标准)
7. **Confidence** (1-5)

Based on the search results, here are the key findings regarding ACL ARR reviewer guidelines for 2025-2026:

## Reviewer Scoring Criteria

Starting with February 2025, ARR reviewers are asked to provide three scores: soundness, excitement, and overall recommendation.

### Soundness Score
The soundness scores must be justified by the text of the review. If you give a low Soundness score without finding any major faults, this means that your review is not a faithful explanation of your recommendation, and you need to revise it.

### Excitement Score
Sometimes you may find the work sound, but not something that is interesting for you personally. Such preferences are more subjective and should be reflected in a separate excitement score, which is orthogonal to the assessment of the soundness of the paper.

_source line 2267 · 2026-05-19T04:05:58.564013+00:00_

# ARR Reviewer Scoring Criteria

Based on the provided guidelines, the document mentions that **as of February 2025, reviewers provide three scores**: soundness, excitement, and overall recommendation. However, the guidelines do not include detailed scoring scales or numerical descriptions for each dimension.

## Available Information

**Three Required Scores:**

1. **Soundness** - The review text must justify this score. Per guidance: "If you give a low Soundness score without finding any major faults, this means that your review is not a faithful explanation of your recommendation."

2. **Excitement** - Described as "more subjective" and "orthogonal to the assessment of the soundness." This reflects personal interest rather than technical merit.

3. **Overall Assessment** - "An explicit recommendation for the outcome of this paper, if it were committed to an *ACL venue." This composite score considers "soundness, excitement, and also other factors like novelty and impact."

## Additional Scoring Elements

The guidelines reference:
- **Confidence score** - Reviewers can indicate confidence levels in their assessment
- **Reproducibility score** - Must be justified in the review text

## Critical Limitation

The document does not provide the actual numerical scales (e.g., 1-5, 1-10), specific score level definitions, or anchors for interpreting what constitutes "low," "medium," or "high" scores on any dimension. These details appear to exist in the referenced "ARR review form" but are not included in these guidelines.

1	\section*{Limitations}
2	\label{sec:limitations}
3	
4	All eight generators are base (pretrained) models.
5	This is a deliberate design choice: base models isolate the generation mechanism from instruction-tuning effects and enable controlled study of recursive distributional drift.
6	However, web text is predominantly produced by instruction-tuned and RLHF-aligned models.
7	Preliminary instruct experiments confirm that depth signal degrades substantially under instruction tuning: OLMo-2-1B-Instruct drops to LOGO F1\,=\,0.338 (vs.\ 0.763 base) and Qwen3-0.6B with chat template to 0.667 (vs.\ 0.816 base); these comparisons use earlier LOGO subsets (5--6 generators) predating the full 8-generator expansion in Table~\ref{tab:logo-results}.
8	Length-matched analysis yields near-zero correlations ($|\rho| < 0.02$) and binary classification near chance (F1\,$\approx$\,0.5) for Qwen models, leaving interpretation ambiguous.
9	Extending depth profiling to instruct models requires addressing these confounds (length regulation, format constraints, safety-driven distribution shaping) that instruction tuning introduces.
10	
11	Our experiments include generators up to 8.2B parameters (Qwen3-8B).
12	Depth signal persists at this scale (Qwen3-8B 3-class F1\,=\,0.714), but cross-scale transfer incurs a moderate gap vs.\ $\leq$2.8B generators (F1\,=\,0.77--0.81).
13	Frontier models (70B+) and closed-source models remain untested; the consistent scale trend (Mistral-7B binary F1\,=\,0.633, lowest among all generators) suggests depth signal may weaken further at frontier scales, potentially approaching the detection floor.
14	
15	Differential depth response strength is prompt-type dependent: continuation chains show strongest drift (within-type binary F1\,=\,0.781) compared to elaboration (0.730) and rewriting (0.737), with within-prompt-type paraphrastic chains showing weaker signal (binary F1\,=\,0.671 vs.\ 0.749 for within-prompt-type continuation).
16	DDR is most reliably detecte

_source line 2309 · 2026-05-19T04:07:12.046436+00:00_

1	\section*{Ethics Statement}
2	\label{sec:ethics}
3	
4	All texts analyzed in this work are LLM-generated from publicly available Wikipedia seeds; no personal or sensitive data is involved.
5	Our method estimates the generation depth of a text passage (how many recursive generation steps it has undergone), not the identity of its author.
6	Individual authorship attribution is outside both the scope and the capability of this approach; privacy implications are minimal.
7	We acknowledge that depth estimation tools could in principle be used to selectively suppress machine-generated text in contexts where such content is legitimate (e.g., accessibility tools, assistive writing).
8	We encourage deployment in data governance pipelines where provenance transparency, rather than content removal, is the objective.
9	All code, data, and trained models will be released upon publication.
10	
11	\paragraph{Licenses.}
12	All generator models are publicly available under permissive licenses (Apache 2.0 for Pythia, OLMo, and Mistral; Qwen License for Qwen3; Llama Community License for Llama 3.2).
13	Wikipedia seed passages are distributed under CC BY-SA 3.0.
14	
15	\paragraph{Compute budget.}
16	All experiments were conducted on 4$\times$ NVIDIA RTX 5090 GPUs (32\,GB each), totaling approximately 62 GPU-hours.
17	
18	\paragraph{Software.}
19	Experiments used Python 3.12.3, PyTorch 2.10.0 (CUDA 12.8), Transformers 4.57.6, XGBoost 3.2.0, scikit-learn 1.8.0, NumPy 2.2.6, pandas 3.0.2, and SciPy 1.17.1.
20
